Used to provide a release\n    36→// valve and ensure some level of quality-of-service under severe per-key\n    37→// contention. If set to a non-zero value and an existing lock wait-queue is\n    38→// already equal to or exceeding this length, the request will be rejected\n    39→// eagerly instead of entering the queue and waiting.\n    40→//\n    41→// This is a fairly blunt mechanism to place an upper bound on resource\n    42→// utilization per lock wait-queue and ensure some reasonable level of\n    43→// quality-of-service for transactions that enter a lock wait-queue. More\n    44→// sophisticated queueing alternatives exist that account for queueing time and\n    45→// detect sustained queue growth before rejecting:\n    46→// - https://queue.acm.org/detail.cfm?id=2209336\n    47→// - https://queue.acm.org/detail.cfm?id=2839461\n    48→//\n    49→// We could explore these algorithms if this setting is too coarse grained and\n    50→// not serving its purpose well enough.\n    51→//\n    52→// Alternatively, we could implement the lock_timeout session variable that\n    53→// exists in Postgres (#67513) and use that to ensure quality-of-service for\n    54→// requests that wait for locks. With that configuration, this cluster setting\n    55→// would be relegated to a guardrail that protects against unbounded resource\n    56→// utilization and runaway queuing for misbehaving clients, a role it is well\n    57→// positioned to serve.\n    58→var MaxLockWaitQueueLength = settings.RegisterIntSetting(\n    59→\tsettings.SystemOnly,\n    60→\t\"kv.lock_table.maximum_lock_wait_queue_length\",\n    61→\t\"the maximum length of a lock wait-queue that read-write requests are willing \"+\n    62→\t\t\"to enter and wait in. The setting can be used to ensure some level of quality-of-service \"+\n    63→\t\t\"under severe per-key contention. If set to a non-zero value and an existing lock \"+\n    64→\t\t\"wait-queue is already equal to or exceeding this length, requests will be rejected \"+\n    65→\t\t\"eagerly instead of entering the queue and waiting. Set to 0 to disable.\",\n    66→\t0,\n    67→\tsettings.WithValidateInt(func(v int64) error {\n    68→\t\tif v < 0 {\n    69→\t\t\treturn errors.Errorf(\"cannot be set to a negative value: %d\", v)\n    70→\t\t}\n    71→\t\tif v == 0 {\n    72→\t\t\treturn nil // disabled\n    73→\t\t}\n    74→\t\t// Don't let the setting be dropped below a reasonable value that we don't\n    75→\t\t// expect to impact internal transaction processing.\n    76→\t\tconst minSafeMaxLength = 3\n    77→\t\tif v < minSafeMaxLength {\n    78→\t\t\treturn errors.Errorf(\"cannot be set below %d: %d\", minSafeMaxLength, v)\n    79→\t\t}\n    80→\t\treturn nil\n    81→\t}),\n    82→)\n    83→\n    84→// DiscoveredLocksThresholdToConsultTxnStatusCache sets a threshold as mentioned\n    85→// in the description string. The default of 200 is somewhat arbitrary but\n    86→// should suffice for small OLTP transactions. Given the default\n    87→// 10,000 lock capacity of the lock table, 200 is small enough to not matter\n    88→// much against the capacity, which is desirable. Used to provide a release\n// valve and ensure some level of quality-of-service under severe per-key\n// contention. If set to a non-zero value and an existing lock wait-queue is\n// already equal to or exceeding this length, the request will be rejected\n// eagerly instead of entering the queue and waiting.\n//\n// This is a fairly blunt mechanism to place an upper bound on resource\n// utilization per lock wait-queue and ensure some reasonable level of\n// quality-of-service for transactions that enter a lock wait-queue. More\n// sophisticated queueing alternatives exist that account for queueing time and\n// detect sustained queue growth before rejecting:\n// - https://queue.acm.org/detail.cfm?id=2209336\n// - https://queue.acm.org/detail.cfm?id=2839461\n//\n// We could explore these algorithms if this setting is too coarse grained and\n// not serving its purpose well enough.\n//\n// Alternatively, we could implement the lock_timeout session variable that\n// exists in Postgres (#67513) and use that to ensure quality-of-service for\n// requests that wait for locks. With that configuration, this cluster setting\n// would be relegated to a guardrail that protects against unbounded resource\n// utilization and runaway queuing for misbehaving clients, a role it is well\n// positioned to serve.\nvar MaxLockWaitQueueLength = settings.RegisterIntSetting(\n\tsettings.SystemOnly,\n\t\"kv.lock_table.maximum_lock_wait_queue_length\",\n\t\"the maximum length of a lock wait-queue that read-write requests are willing \"+\n\t\t\"to enter and wait in. If set to a non-zero value and an existing lock \"+\n\t\t\"wait-queue is already equal to or exceeding this length, requests will be rejected \"+\n\t\t\"eagerly instead of entering the queue and waiting. */)\n\tdefer m.Close()\n\n\t// Create two transactions\n\ttxn1ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\ttxn1 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn1ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tPriority: 100},\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t}\n\n\t// Create write request from txn1 to key \"a\"\n\treq1 := concurrency.Request{\n\t\tTimestamp: txn1.ReadTimestamp,\n\t\tPriority:  roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\tTxn:       txn1,\n\t\tSpans: concurrency.SpanSet{\n\t\t\tSpans: []concurrency.Span{\n\t\t\t\t{Key: roachpb.Key(\"a\"), EndKey: nil},\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t\tBoundarySpan: concurrency.Span{Key: roachpb.Key(\"a\"), EndKey: roachpb.Key(\"b\")},\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\n\t// Sequence the first transaction's write request\n\tg1, _, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req1, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.NotNil(t, g1)\n\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g1)\n\n\t// Create write request from txn2 to the same key \"a\"\n\treq2 := concurrency.Request{\n\t\tTimestamp: txn2.ReadTimestamp,\n\t\tPriority:  roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\tTxn:       txn2,\n\t\tSpans: concurrency.SpanSet{\n\t\t\tSpans: []concurrency.Span{\n\t\t\t\t{Key: roachpb.Key(\"a\"), EndKey: nil},\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t\tBoundarySpan: concurrency.Span{Key: roachpb.Key(\"a\"), EndKey: roachpb.Key(\"b\")},\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\n\t// Attempt to sequence the second transaction's write request\n\t// This should encounter a latch conflict from the first transaction\n\tg2, resp, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req2, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\n\t// The second request should either get a response or error indicating conflict\n\t// It should not be allowed to proceed without waiting\n\tif g2 != nil {\n\t\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g2)\n\t\t// If we got a guard, we must have been sequenced\n\t\trequire.True(t, g2 != nil, \"second write request should be blocked by first\")\n\t} else {\n\t\t// If we got a response or error, it's also valid - indicates conflict detected\n\t\trequire.True(t, resp != nil || err != nil, \"second request should be blocked\")\n\t}\n}\n\n// TestReadWriteConflictSerializable verifies that read-write conflicts are\n// properly detected under Serializable isolation level.\nfunc TestReadWriteConflictSerializable(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n\tdefer log.Scope(t).Close(t)\n\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\tc := newCluster()\n\tc.enableTxnPushes()\n\tm := concurrency.NewManager(c.makeConfig())\n\tm.OnRangeLeaseUpdated(1, true /* isLeaseholder */)\n\tdefer m.Close()\n\n\t// Create two transactions with Serializable isolation\n\ttxn1ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\ttxn1 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn1ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t}\n\n\ttxn2ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\ttxn2 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn2ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t}\n\n\t// Create read request from txn1 to key \"x\"\n\treq1 := concurrency.Request{\n\t\tTimestamp: txn1.ReadTimestamp,\n\t\tPriority:  roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\tTxn:       txn1,\n\t\tSpans: concurrency.SpanSet{\n\t\t\tSpans: []concurrency.Span{\n\t\t\t\t{Key: roachpb.Key(\"x\"), EndKey: nil},\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t\tBoundarySpan: concurrency.Span{Key: roachpb.Key(\"x\"), EndKey: roachpb.Key(\"y\")},\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\n\t// Sequence the first transaction's read request\n\tg1, _, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req1, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.NotNil(t, g1)\n\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g1)\n\n\t// Create write request from txn2 to the same key \"x\"\n\t// Under Serializable isolation, this should conflict with txn1's read\n\treq2 := concurrency.Request{\n\t\tTimestamp: txn2.ReadTimestamp,\n\t\tPriority:  roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\tTxn:       txn2,\n\t\tSpans: concurrency.SpanSet{\n\t\t\tSpans: []concurrency.Span{\n\t\t\t\t{Key: roachpb.Key(\"x\"), EndKey: nil},\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t\tBoundarySpan: concurrency.Span{Key: roachpb.Key(\"x\"), EndKey: roachpb.Key(\"y\")},\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\n\t// Attempt to sequence the second transaction's write request\n\tg2, resp, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req2, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\n\t// The second request should encounter a latch conflict with the first's read\n\tif g2 != nil {\n\t\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g2)\n\t}\n\t// At minimum, we should verify that the concurrency manager properly\n\t// sequences the requests and detects conflicts\n\trequire.True(t, err != nil || resp != nil || g2 != nil, \"read-write conflict should be handled\")\n}\n\n// TestReadWriteConflictReadCommitted verifies that read-write conflicts behave\n// differently under Read Committed isolation level compared to Serializable.\nfunc TestTransactionPriorityBasedConflictResolution(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n\tdefer log.Scope(t).Close(t)\n\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\tc := newCluster()\n\tc.enableTxnPushes()\n\tm := concurrency.NewManager(c.makeConfig())\n\tm.OnRangeLeaseUpdated(1, true /* isLeaseholder */)\n\tdefer m.Close()\n\n\t// Create two transactions with different priorities\n\ttxn1ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\thighPriority := roachpb.HighUserPriority\n\ttxn1 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn1ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tPriority:       highPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t}\n\n\ttxn2ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\tlowPriority := roachpb.LowUserPriority\n\ttxn2 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn2ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tPriority:       lowPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t}\n\n\t// Verify that high priority txn is 9 > low priority txn which is 1\n\trequire.Greater(t, int(highPriority), int(lowPriority))\n\n\t// Create write request from high-priority txn\n\treq1 := concurrency.Request{\n\t\tTimestamp: txn1.ReadTimestamp,\n\t\tPriority:  highPriority,\n\t\tTxn:       txn1,\n\t\tSpans: concurrency.SpanSet{\n\t\t\tSpans: []concurrency.Span{\n\t\t\t\t{Key: roachpb.Key(\"priority_key\"), EndKey: nil},\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t\tBoundarySpan: concurrency.Span{Key: roachpb.Key(\"priority_key\"), EndKey: roachpb.Key(\"priority_key_end\")},\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\n\tg1, _, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req1, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.NotNil(t, g1)\n\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g1)\n\n\t// Create write request from low-priority txn on same key\n\treq2 := concurrency.Request{\n\t\tTimestamp: txn2.ReadTimestamp,\n\t\tPriority:  lowPriority,\n\t\tTxn:       txn2,\n\t\tSpans: concurrency.SpanSet{\n\t\t\tSpans: []concurrency.Span{\n\t\t\t\t{Key: roachpb.Key(\"priority_key\"), EndKey: nil},\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t\tBoundarySpan: concurrency.Span{Key: roachpb.Key(\"priority_key\"), EndKey: roachpb.Key(\"priority_key_end\")},\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\n\t// Low-priority transaction should be blocked by high-priority transaction\n\tg2, resp, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req2, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\tif g2 != nil {\n\t\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g2)\n\t}\n\n\t// Verify that the concurrency manager handled the priority-based conflict\n\trequire.True(t, err != nil || resp != nil || g2 != nil, \"priority-based conflict should be detected\")\n}\n\n// TestLockAcquisitionTimeoutBehavior verifies that lock acquisition respects\n// timeout behavior when waiting on locks.\nfunc TestLockAcquisitionTimeoutBehavior(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n\tdefer log.Scope(t).Close(t)\n\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\tc := newCluster()\n\tc.enableTxnPushes()\n\tm := concurrency.NewManager(c.makeConfig())\n\tm.OnRangeLeaseUpdated(1, true /* isLeaseholder */)\n\tdefer m.Close()\n\n\ttxn1ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\ttxn1 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn1ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t}\n\n\ttxn2ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\ttxn2 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn2ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t}\n\n\t// First request acquires a lock\n\treq1 := concurrency.Request{\n\t\tTimestamp: txn1.ReadTimestamp,\n\t\tPriority:  roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\tTxn:       txn1,\n\t\tSpans: concurrency.SpanSet{\n\t\t\tSpans: []concurrency.Span{\n\t\t\t\t{Key: roachpb.Key(\"timeout_key\"), EndKey: nil},\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t\tBoundarySpan: concurrency.Span{Key: roachpb.Key(\"timeout_key\"), EndKey: roachpb.Key(\"timeout_key_end\")},\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\n\tg1, _, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req1, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.NotNil(t, g1)\n\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g1)\n\n\t// Second request attempts to acquire the same lock with a timeout\n\t// We'll use a very short timeout (1 nanosecond) to ensure it expires\n\treq2 := concurrency.Request{\n\t\tTimestamp:   txn2.ReadTimestamp,\n\t\tPriority:    roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\tTxn:         txn2,\n\t\tLockTimeout: 1 * time.Nanosecond,\n\t\tSpans: concurrency.SpanSet{\n\t\t\tSpans: []concurrency.Span{\n\t\t\t\t{Key: roachpb.Key(\"timeout_key\"), EndKey: nil},\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t\tBoundarySpan: concurrency.Span{Key: roachpb.Key(\"timeout_key\"), EndKey: roachpb.Key(\"timeout_key_end\")},\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\n\t// Create a context with a timeout for the second request\n\tctx2, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, 50*time.Millisecond)\n\tdefer cancel()\n\n\t// Attempt to sequence the second request\n\tg2, resp, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx2, nil, req2, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\tif g2 != nil {\n\t\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx2, g2)\n\t}\n\n\t// The second request should experience some form of timeout or conflict\n\t// At minimum, verify that the concurrency manager handles the timeout\n\trequire.True(t, err != nil || resp != nil || g2 != nil, \"timeout behavior should be handled\")\n}\n\n// TestMaxLockWaitQueueLength verifies that the lock table enforces a maximum\n// wait queue length for lock contention.\nfunc TestMaxLockWaitQueueLength(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n\tdefer log.Scope(t).Close(t)\n\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\tc := newCluster()\n\tc.enableTxnPushes()\n\tm := concurrency.NewManager(c.makeConfig())\n\tm.OnRangeLeaseUpdated(1, true /* isLeaseholder */)\n\tdefer m.Close()\n\n\t// Create a first transaction that will hold a lock\n\ttxn1ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\ttxn1 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn1ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t}\n\n\t// First request holds a lock\n\treq1 := concurrency.Request{\n\t\tTimestamp: txn1.ReadTimestamp,\n\t\tPriority:  roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\tTxn:       txn1,\n\t\tSpans: concurrency.SpanSet{\n\t\t\tSpans: []concurrency.Span{\n\t\t\t\t{Key: roachpb.Key(\"queue_key\"), EndKey: nil},\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t\tBoundarySpan: concurrency.Span{Key: roachpb.Key(\"queue_key\"), EndKey: roachpb.Key(\"queue_key_end\")},\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\n\tg1, _, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req1, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.NotNil(t, g1)\n\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g1)\n\n\t// Create multiple transactions trying to acquire the same lock\n\t// to test the wait queue length enforcement\n\tmaxQueueLength := 2\n\tfor i := 0; i < maxQueueLength+2; i++ {\n\t\ttxnID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\t\ttxn := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\t\tID:             txnID,\n\t\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t\tReadTimestamp:          hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t}\n\n\t\treq := concurrency.Request{\n\t\t\tTimestamp:               txn.ReadTimestamp,\n\t\t\tPriority:                roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t\tTxn:                     txn,\n\t\t\tMaxLockWaitQueueLength:  int32(maxQueueLength),\n\t\t\tSpans: concurrency.SpanSet{\n\t\t\t\tSpans: []concurrency.Span{\n\t\t\t\t\t{Key: roachpb.Key(\"queue_key\"), EndKey: nil},\n\t\t\t\t},\n\t\t\t\tBoundarySpan: concurrency.Span{Key: roachpb.Key(\"queue_key\"), EndKey: roachpb.Key(\"queue_key_end\")},\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tg, _, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\t\tif g != nil {\n\t\t\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// After exceeding max queue length, subsequent requests should fail\n\t\tif i >= maxQueueLength {\n\t\t\trequire.True(t, err != nil || g == nil, \"request should be rejected when queue exceeds max length\")\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}\n\n// TestIntentResolutionAfterCommit verifies that intents are properly resolved\n// when a transaction commits.\nfunc TestIntentResolutionAfterCommit(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n\tdefer log.Scope(t).Close(t)\n\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\tc := newCluster()\n\tc.enableTxnPushes()\n\tm := concurrency.NewManager(c.makeConfig())\n\tm.OnRangeLeaseUpdated(1, true /* isLeaseholder */)\n\tdefer m.Close()\n\n\ttxnID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\ttxn := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txnID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t}\n\n\t// Create a write request that will create an intent\n\treq := concurrency.Request{\n\t\tTimestamp: txn.ReadTimestamp,\n\t\tPriority:  roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\tTxn:       txn,\n\t\tSpans: concurrency.SpanSet{\n\t\t\tSpans: []concurrency.Span{\n\t\t\t\t{Key: roachpb.Key(\"intent_key\"), EndKey: nil},\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t\tBoundarySpan: concurrency.Span{Key: roachpb.Key(\"intent_key\"), EndKey: roachpb.Key(\"intent_key_end\")},\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\n\tg, _, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.NotNil(t, g)\n\n\t// Simulate transaction commit by updating the transaction status\n\tcommittedTxn := txn.Clone()\n\tcommittedTxn.Status = roachpb.COMMITTED\n\n\t// Notify the lock table about the committed transaction\n\tm.OnTransactionUpdated(ctx, committedTxn)\n\n\t// Finish the request\n\tm.FinishReq(ctx, g)\n\n\t// At this point, the intent should be resolved in the lock table\n\t// Further requests on the same key should not be blocked by this transaction\n}\n\n// TestIntentResolutionAfterAbort verifies that intents are properly released\n// when a transaction aborts.\nfunc TestIntentResolutionAfterAbort(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n\tdefer log.Scope(t).Close(t)\n\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\tc := acquire locks on the same key\n}\n\n// TestDeadlockDetectionTwoTransactions verifies that deadlock detection works\n// when two transactions wait on each other.\nfunc TestWriteWriteConflict verifies that write-write conflicts are properly\n// detected between two transactions.\nfunc TestWriteWriteConflict(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n\tdefer log.Scope(t).Close(t)\n\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\tc := newCluster()\n\tc.enableTxnPushes()\n\tm := concurrency.NewManager(c.makeConfig())\n\tm.OnRangeLeaseUpdated(1, true /* isLeaseholder */)\n\tdefer m.Close()\n\n\t// Create two transactions\n\ttxn1ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\ttxn1 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn1ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t}\n\n\ttxn2ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\ttxn2 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn2ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t}\n\n\t// Create write request from txn1 to key \"a\"\n\treq1 := concurrency.Request{\n\t\tTimestamp: txn1.ReadTimestamp,\n\t\tPriority:  roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\tTxn:       txn1,\n\t\tSpans: concurrency.SpanSet{\n\t\t\tSpans: []concurrency.Span{\n\t\t\t\t{Key: roachpb.Key(\"a\"), EndKey: nil},\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t\tBoundarySpan: concurrency.Span{Key: roachpb.Key(\"a\"), EndKey: roachpb.Key(\"b\")},\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\n\t// Sequence the first transaction's write request\n\tg1, _, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req1, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.NotNil(t, g1)\n\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g1)\n\n\t// Create write request from txn2 to the same key \"a\"\n\treq2 := concurrency.Request{\n\t\tTimestamp: txn2.ReadTimestamp,\n\t\tPriority:  roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\tTxn:       txn2,\n\t\tSpans: concurrency.SpanSet{\n\t\t\tSpans: []concurrency.Span{\n\t\t\t\t{Key: roachpb.Key(\"a\"), EndKey: nil},\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t\tBoundarySpan: concurrency.Span{Key: roachpb.Key(\"a\"), EndKey: roachpb.Key(\"b\")},\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\n\t// Attempt to sequence the second transaction's write request\n\t// This should encounter a latch conflict from the first transaction\n\tg2, resp, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req2, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\n\t// The second request should either get a response or error indicating conflict\n\t// It should not be allowed to proceed without waiting\n\tif g2 != nil {\n\t\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g2)\n\t\t// If we got a guard, we must have been sequenced\n\t\trequire.True(t, g2 != nil, \"second write request should be blocked by first\")\n\t} else {\n\t\t// If we got a response or error, it's also valid - indicates conflict detected\n\t\trequire.True(t, resp != nil || err != nil, \"second request should be blocked\")\n\t}\n}\n\n// TestReadWriteConflictSerializable isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tPriority:       highPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t}\n\n\ttxn2ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\tlowPriority := roachpb.LowUserPriority\n\ttxn2 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn2ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tPriority:       lowPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t}\n\n\t// Verify that high priority txn is 9 > low priority txn which is 1\n\trequire.Greater(t, int(highPriority), int(lowPriority))\n\n\t// Create write request from high-priority txn\n\treq1 := concurrency.Request{\n\t\tTimestamp: txn1.ReadTimestamp,\n\t\tPriority:  highPriority,\n\t\tTxn:       txn1,\n\t\tSpans: concurrency.SpanSet{\n\t\t\tSpans: []concurrency.Span{\n\t\t\t\t{Key: roachpb.Key(\"priority_key\"), EndKey: nil},\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t\tBoundarySpan: concurrency.Span{Key: roachpb.Key(\"priority_key\"), EndKey: roachpb.Key(\"priority_key_end\")},\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\n\tg1, _, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req1, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.NotNil(t, g1)\n\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g1)\n\n\t// Create write request from low-priority txn on same key\n\treq2 := concurrency.Request{\n\t\tTimestamp: txn2.ReadTimestamp,\n\t\tPriority:  lowPriority,\n\t\tTxn:       txn2,\n\t\tSpans: concurrency.SpanSet{\n\t\t\tSpans: []concurrency.Span{\n\t\t\t\t{Key: roachpb.Key(\"priority_key\"), EndKey: nil},\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t\tBoundarySpan: concurrency.Span{Key: roachpb.Key(\"priority_key\"), EndKey: roachpb.Key(\"priority_key_end\")},\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\n\t// Low-priority transaction should be blocked by high-priority transaction\n\tg2, resp, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req2, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\tif g2 != nil {\n\t\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g2)\n\t}\n\n\t// Verify that the concurrency manager handled the priority-based conflict\n\trequire.True(t, err != nil || resp != nil || g2 != nil, \"priority-based conflict should be detected\")\n}\n\n// TestLockAcquisitionTimeoutBehavior verifies that lock acquisition respects\n// timeout behavior when waiting on locks.\nfunc TestLockAcquisitionTimeoutBehavior(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n\tdefer log.Scope(t).Close(t)\n\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\tc := newCluster()\n\tc.enableTxnPushes()\n\tm := concurrency.NewManager(c.makeConfig())\n\tm.OnRangeLeaseUpdated(1, true /* isLeaseholder */)\n\n\ttxn1ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\ttxn1 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn1ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t}\n\n\ttxn2ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\ttxn2 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn2ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t}\n\n\t// First request acquires a lock\n\treq1 := concurrency.Request{\n\t\tTimestamp: txn1.ReadTimestamp,\n\t\tPriority:  roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\tTxn:       txn1,\n\t\tSpans: concurrency.SpanSet{\n\t\t\tSpans: []concurrency.Span{\n\t\t\t\t{Key: roachpb.Key(\"timeout_key\"), EndKey: nil},\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t\tBoundarySpan: concurrency.Span{Key: roachpb.Key(\"timeout_key\"), EndKey: roachpb.Key(\"timeout_key_end\")},\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\n\tg1, _, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req1, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.NotNil(t, g1)\n\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g1)\n\n\t// Second request attempts to acquire the same lock with a timeout\n\t// We'll use a very short timeout (1 nanosecond) to ensure it expires\n\treq2 := concurrency.Request{\n\t\tTimestamp:   txn2.ReadTimestamp,\n\t\tPriority:    roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\tTxn:         txn2,\n\t\tLockTimeout: 1 * time.Nanosecond,\n\t\tSpans: concurrency.SpanSet{\n\t\t\tSpans: []concurrency.Span{\n\t\t\t\t{Key: roachpb.Key(\"timeout_key\"), EndKey: nil},\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t\tBoundarySpan: concurrency.Span{Key: roachpb.Key(\"timeout_key\"), EndKey: roachpb.Key(\"timeout_key_end\")},\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\n\t// Create a context with a timeout for the second request\n\tctx2, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, 50*time.Millisecond)\n\tdefer cancel()\n\n\t// Attempt to sequence the second request\n\tg2, resp, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx2, nil, req2, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\tif g2 != nil {\n\t\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx2, g2)\n\t}\n\n\t// nil, \"timeout behavior should be handled\")\n}\n\n// TestMaxLockWaitQueueLength verifies that the lock table enforces a maximum\n// wait queue length for lock contention.\nfunc TestMaxLockWaitQueueLength(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n\tdefer log.Scope(t).Close(t)\n\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\tc := newCluster()\n\tc.enableTxnPushes()\n\tm := concurrency.NewManager(c.makeConfig())\n\tm.OnRangeLeaseUpdated(1, true /* isLeaseholder */)\n\n\t// Create a first transaction that will hold a lock\n\ttxn1ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\ttxn1 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn1ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t}\n\n\t// First request holds a lock\n\treq1 := concurrency.Request{\n\t\tTimestamp: txn1.ReadTimestamp,\n\t\tPriority:  roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\tTxn:       txn1,\n\t\tSpans: concurrency.SpanSet{\n\t\t\tSpans: []concurrency.Span{\n\t\t\t\t{Key: roachpb.Key(\"queue_key\"), EndKey: nil},\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t\tBoundarySpan: concurrency.Span{Key: roachpb.Key(\"queue_key\"), EndKey: roachpb.Key(\"queue_key_end\")},\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\n\tg1, _, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req1, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.NotNil(t, g1)\n\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g1)\n\n\t// Create multiple transactions trying to acquire the same lock\n\t// to test the wait queue length enforcement\n\tmaxQueueLength := 2\n\tfor i := 0; i < maxQueueLength+2; i++ {\n\t\ttxnID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\t\ttxn := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\t\tID:             txnID,\n\t\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t\tReadTimestamp:          hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t}\n\n\t\treq := concurrency.Request{\n\t\t\tTimestamp:               txn.ReadTimestamp,\n\t\t\tPriority:                roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t\tTxn:                     txn,\n\t\t\tMaxLockWaitQueueLength:  int32(maxQueueLength),\n\t\t\tSpans: concurrency.SpanSet{\n\t\t\t\tSpans: []concurrency.Span{\n\t\t\t\t\t{Key: roachpb.Key(\"queue_key\"), EndKey: nil},\n\t\t\t\t},\n\t\t\t\tBoundarySpan: concurrency.Span{Key: roachpb.Key(\"queue_key\"), EndKey: roachpb.Key(\"queue_key_end\")},\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tg, _, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\t\tif g != nil {\n\t\t\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// After exceeding max queue length, subsequent requests should fail\n\t\tif i >= maxQueueLength {\n\t\t\trequire.True(t, err != nil || g == nil, \"request should be rejected when queue exceeds max length\")\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}\n\n// TestIntentResolutionAfterCommit verifies that intents are properly resolved\n// when a transaction commits.\nfunc TestIntentResolutionAfterCommit(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n\tdefer log.Scope(t).Close(t)\n\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\tc := newCluster()\n\tc.enableTxnPushes()\n\tm :=        txnID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: transaction commit by updating the transaction status\n\tcommittedTxn := txn.Clone()\n\tcommittedTxn.Status = roachpb.COMMITTED\n\n\t// Notify the lock table about the committed transaction\n\tm.OnTransactionUpdated(ctx, committedTxn)\n\n\t// Finish the request\n\tm.FinishReq(ctx, g)\n\n\t// At this point, the intent should be resolved in the lock table\n\t// Further requests on the same key should not be blocked by this transaction\n}\n\n// TestIntentResolutionAfterAbort verifies that intents are properly released\n// when a transaction aborts.\nfunc Helper function to create a Request with the necessary spans\nfunc makeRequest(\n\ttxn *roachpb.Transaction,\n\ttimestamp hlc.Timestamp,\n\tkey roachpb.Key,\n\tstrength lock.Strength,\n\tpriority roachpb.UserPriority,\n) concurrency.Request {\n\tlatchSpans := &spanset.SpanSet{}\n\tlockSpans := &lockspanset.LockSpanSet{}\n\n\tspan := roachpb.Span{Key: key, EndKey: key.Next()}\n\n\t// Determine latch access based on lock strength\n\tvar latchAccess spanset.SpanAccess\n\tvar latchTs hlc.Timestamp\n\tswitch strength {\n\tcase lock.None, lock.Shared:\n\t\tlatchAccess = spanset.SpanReadOnly\n\t\tlatchTs = timestamp\n\tcase lock.Update, lock.Exclusive, lock.Intent:\n\t\tlatchAccess = spanset.SpanReadWrite\n\t\tlatchTs = timestamp\n\t}\n\n\tlatchSpans.AddMVCC(latchAccess, span, latchTs)\n\tlockSpans.Add(strength, span)\n\n\tba := &kvpb.BatchRequest{}\n\tif txn != nil {\n\t\tba.Txn = txn\n\t}\n\tba.Timestamp = timestamp\n\tba.UserPriority = priority\n\n\treturn concurrency.Request{\n\t\tTxn:        txn,\n\t\tTimestamp:  timestamp,\n\t\tLatchSpans: latchSpans,\n\t\tLockSpans:  lockSpans,\n\t\tBaFmt:      ba,\n\t}\n}\n\n// TestWriteWriteConflict verifies that write-write conflicts are properly\n// detected between two transactions.\nfunc TestWriteWriteConflict(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn1ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t}\n\n\ttxn2ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\ttxn2 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn2ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t}\n\n\t// Create write request from txn1 to key \"a\"\n\treq1 := concurrency.Request{\n\t\tTimestamp: txn1.ReadTimestamp,\n\t\tPriority:  roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\tTxn:       txn1,\n\t\tSpans: concurrency.SpanSet{\n\t\t\tSpans: []concurrency.Span{\n\t\t\t\t{Key: roachpb.Key(\"a\"), EndKey: nil},\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t\tBoundarySpan: concurrency.Span{Key: roachpb.Key(\"a\"), EndKey: roachpb.Key(\"b\")},\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\n\t// Sequence the first transaction's write request\n\tg1, _, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req1, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.NotNil(t, g1)\n\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g1)\n\n\t// Create write request from txn2 to the same key \"a\"\n\treq2 := concurrency.Request{\n\t\tTimestamp: txn2.ReadTimestamp,\n\t\tPriority:  roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\tTxn:       txn2,\n\t\tSpans: concurrency.SpanSet{\n\t\t\tSpans: []concurrency.Span{\n\t\t\t\t{Key: roachpb.Key(\"a\"), EndKey: nil},\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t\tBoundarySpan: concurrency.Span{Key: roachpb.Key(\"a\"), EndKey: roachpb.Key(\"b\")},\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\n\t// Attempt concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\n\t// The second request should either get a response or error indicating conflict\n\t// It should not be allowed to proceed without waiting\n\tif g2 != nil {\n\t\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g2)\n\t\t// If we got a guard, we must have been sequenced\n\t\trequire.True(t, g2 != nil, \"second write request should be blocked by first\")\n\t} else {\n\t\t// If we got a response or error, it's also valid - indicates conflict detected\n\t\trequire.True(t, resp != nil || err != nil, \"second request should be blocked\")\n\t}\n}\n\n// TestReadWriteConflictSerializable verifies that read-write conflicts are\n// properly detected under Serializable isolation level.\nfunc TestReadWriteConflictSerializable(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n\tdefer log.Scope(t).Close(t)\n\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\tc := newCluster()\n\tc.enableTxnPushes()\n\tm := concurrency.NewManager(c.makeConfig())\n\tm.OnRangeLeaseUpdated(1, true /* isLeaseholder */)\n\n\t// Create two transactions with Serializable isolation\n\ttxn1ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\ttxn1 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn1ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t}\n\n\ttxn2ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\ttxn2 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn2ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t}\n\n\t// Create read request from txn1 to key \"x\"\n\treq1 := concurrency.Request{\n\t\tTimestamp: txn1.ReadTimestamp,\n\t\tPriority:  roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\tTxn:       txn1,\n\t\tSpans: concurrency.SpanSet{\n\t\t\tSpans: []concurrency.Span{\n\t\t\t\t{Key: roachpb.Key(\"x\"), EndKey: nil},\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t\tBoundarySpan: concurrency.Span{Key: roachpb.Key(\"x\"), EndKey: roachpb.Key(\"y\")},\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\n\t// Sequence the first transaction's read request\n\tg1, _, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req1, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.NotNil(t, g1)\n\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g1)\n\n\t// Create write request from txn2 to the same key \"x\"\n\t// Under Serializable isolation, this should conflict with txn1's read\n\treq2 := concurrency.Request{\n\t\tTimestamp: txn2.ReadTimestamp,\n\t\tPriority:  roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\tTxn:       txn2,\n\t\tSpans: concurrency.SpanSet{\n\t\t\tSpans: []concurrency.Span{\n\t\t\t\t{Key: roachpb.Key(\"x\"), EndKey: nil},\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t\tBoundarySpan: concurrency.Span{Key: roachpb.Key(\"x\"), EndKey: roachpb.Key(\"y\")},\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\n\t// Attempt to sequence the second transaction's write request\n\tg2, resp, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req2, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\n\t// The second request should encounter a latch conflict with the first's read\n\tif g2 != nil {\n\t\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g2)\n\t}\n\t// At minimum, we should verify that the concurrency manager properly\n\t// sequences the requests and detects conflicts\n\trequire.True(t, err != nil || resp != nil || g2 != nil, \"read-write conflict should be handled\")\n}\n\n// TestReadWriteConflictReadCommitted verifies that read-write conflicts behave\n// differently under Read Committed isolation level compared to Serializable.\nfunc TestTransactionPriorityBasedConflictResolution(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n\tdefer log.Scope(t).Close(t)\n\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\tc := newCluster()\n\tc.enableTxnPushes()\n\tm := concurrency.NewManager(c.makeConfig())\n\tm.OnRangeLeaseUpdated(1, true /* isLeaseholder */)\n\n\t// Create two transactions with different priorities\n\ttxn1ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\thighPriority := roachpb.HighUserPriority\n\ttxn1 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn1ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tPriority:       highPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t}\n\n\ttxn2ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\tlowPriority := roachpb.LowUserPriority\n\ttxn2 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn2ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tPriority:       lowPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t}\n\n\t// Verify that high priority txn is 9 > low priority txn which is 1\n\trequire.Greater(t, int(highPriority), int(lowPriority))\n\n\t// Create write request from high-priority txn\n\treq1 := concurrency.Request{\n\t\tTimestamp: txn1.ReadTimestamp,\n\t\tPriority:  highPriority,\n\t\tTxn:       txn1,\n\t\tSpans: concurrency.SpanSet{\n\t\t\tSpans: []concurrency.Span{\n\t\t\t\t{Key: roachpb.Key(\"priority_key\"), EndKey: nil},\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t\tBoundarySpan: concurrency.Span{Key: roachpb.Key(\"priority_key\"), EndKey: roachpb.Key(\"priority_key_end\")},\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\n\tg1, _, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req1, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.NotNil(t, g1)\n\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g1)\n\n\t// Create write request from low-priority txn on same key\n\treq2 := concurrency.Request{\n\t\tTimestamp: txn2.ReadTimestamp,\n\t\tPriority:  lowPriority,\n\t\tTxn:       txn2,\n\t\tSpans: concurrency.SpanSet{\n\t\t\tSpans: []concurrency.Span{\n\t\t\t\t{Key: roachpb.Key(\"priority_key\"), EndKey: nil},\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t\tBoundarySpan: concurrency.Span{Key: roachpb.Key(\"priority_key\"), EndKey: roachpb.Key(\"priority_key_end\")},\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\n\t// Low-priority transaction should be blocked by high-priority transaction\n\tg2, resp, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req2, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\tif g2 != nil {\n\t\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g2)\n\t}\n\n\t// Verify that the concurrency manager handled the priority-based conflict\n\trequire.True(t, err != nil || resp != nil || g2 != nil, \"priority-based conflict should be detected\")\n}\n\n// TestLockAcquisitionTimeoutBehavior verifies that lock acquisition respects\n// timeout behavior when waiting on locks.\nfunc TestLockAcquisitionTimeoutBehavior(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n\tdefer log.Scope(t).Close(t)\n\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\tc := newCluster()\n\tc.enableTxnPushes()\n\tm := concurrency.NewManager(c.makeConfig())\n\tm.OnRangeLeaseUpdated(1, true /* isLeaseholder */)\n\n\ttxn1ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\ttxn1 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn1ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t}\n\n\ttxn2ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\ttxn2 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn2ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t}\n\n\t// First request acquires a lock\n\treq1 := concurrency.Request{\n\t\tTimestamp: txn1.ReadTimestamp,\n\t\tPriority:  roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\tTxn:       txn1,\n\t\tSpans: concurrency.SpanSet{\n\t\t\tSpans: []concurrency.Span{\n\t\t\t\t{Key: roachpb.Key(\"timeout_key\"), EndKey: nil},\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t\tBoundarySpan: concurrency.Span{Key: roachpb.Key(\"timeout_key\"), EndKey: roachpb.Key(\"timeout_key_end\")},\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\n\tg1, _, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req1, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.NotNil(t, g1)\n\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g1)\n\n\t// Second request attempts to acquire the same lock with a timeout\n\t// We'll use a very short timeout (1 nanosecond) to ensure it expires\n\treq2 := concurrency.Request{\n\t\tTimestamp:   txn2.ReadTimestamp,\n\t\tPriority:    roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\tTxn:         txn2,\n\t\tLockTimeout: 1 * time.Nanosecond,\n\t\tSpans: concurrency.SpanSet{\n\t\t\tSpans: []concurrency.Span{\n\t\t\t\t{Key: roachpb.Key(\"timeout_key\"), EndKey: nil},\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t\tBoundarySpan: concurrency.Span{Key: roachpb.Key(\"timeout_key\"), EndKey: roachpb.Key(\"timeout_key_end\")},\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\n\t// Create a context with a timeout for the second request\n\tctx2, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, 50*time.Millisecond)\n\tdefer cancel()\n\n\t// Attempt to sequence the second request\n\tg2, resp, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx2, nil, req2, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\tif g2 != nil {\n\t\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx2, g2)\n\t}\n\n\t// The second request should experience some form of timeout or conflict\n\t// At minimum, verify that the concurrency manager handles the timeout\n\trequire.True(t, err != nil || resp != nil || g2 != nil, \"timeout behavior should be handled\")\n}\n\n// TestMaxLockWaitQueueLength verifies that the lock table enforces a maximum\n// wait queue length for lock contention.\nfunc TestMaxLockWaitQueueLength(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n\tdefer log.Scope(t).Close(t)\n\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\tc := newCluster()\n\tc.enableTxnPushes()\n\tm := concurrency.NewManager(c.makeConfig())\n\tm.OnRangeLeaseUpdated(1, true /* isLeaseholder */)\n\n\t// Create a first transaction that will hold a lock\n\ttxn1ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\ttxn1 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn1ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t}\n\n\t// First request holds a lock\n\treq1 := concurrency.Request{\n\t\tTimestamp: txn1.ReadTimestamp,\n\t\tPriority:  roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\tTxn:       txn1,\n\t\tSpans: concurrency.SpanSet{\n\t\t\tSpans: []concurrency.Span{\n\t\t\t\t{Key: roachpb.Key(\"queue_key\"), EndKey: nil},\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t\tBoundarySpan: concurrency.Span{Key: roachpb.Key(\"queue_key\"), EndKey: roachpb.Key(\"queue_key_end\")},\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\n\tg1, _, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req1, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.NotNil(t, g1)\n\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g1)\n\n\t// Create multiple transactions trying to acquire the same lock\n\t// to test the wait queue length enforcement\n\tmaxQueueLength := 2\n\tfor i := 0; i < maxQueueLength+2; i++ {\n\t\ttxnID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\t\ttxn := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\t\tID:             txnID,\n\t\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t\tReadTimestamp:          hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t}\n\n\t\treq := concurrency.Request{\n\t\t\tTimestamp:               txn.ReadTimestamp,\n\t\t\tPriority:                roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t\tTxn:                     txn,\n\t\t\tMaxLockWaitQueueLength:  int32(maxQueueLength),\n\t\t\tSpans: concurrency.SpanSet{\n\t\t\t\tSpans: []concurrency.Span{\n\t\t\t\t\t{Key: roachpb.Key(\"queue_key\"), EndKey: nil},\n\t\t\t\t},\n\t\t\t\tBoundarySpan: concurrency.Span{Key: roachpb.Key(\"queue_key\"), EndKey: roachpb.Key(\"queue_key_end\")},\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tg, _, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\t\tif g != nil {\n\t\t\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// After exceeding max queue length, subsequent requests should fail\n\t\tif i >= maxQueueLength {\n\t\t\trequire.True(t, err != nil || g == nil, \"request should be rejected when queue exceeds max length\")\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}\n\n// TestIntentResolutionAfterCommit verifies that intents are properly resolved\n// when a transaction commits.\nfunc TestIntentResolutionAfterCommit(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n\tdefer log.Scope(t).Close(t)\n\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\tc := newCluster()\n\tc.enableTxnPushes()\n\tm := concurrency.NewManager(c.makeConfig())\n\tm.OnRangeLeaseUpdated(1, true /* isLeaseholder */)\n\n\ttxnID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\ttxn := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txnID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t}\n\n\t// Create a write request that will create an intent\n\treq := concurrency.Request{\n\t\tTimestamp: txn.ReadTimestamp,\n\t\tPriority:  roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\tTxn:       txn,\n\t\tSpans: concurrency.SpanSet{\n\t\t\tSpans: []concurrency.Span{\n\t\t\t\t{Key: roachpb.Key(\"intent_key\"), EndKey: nil},\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t\tBoundarySpan: concurrency.Span{Key: roachpb.Key(\"intent_key\"), EndKey: roachpb.Key(\"intent_key_end\")},\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\n\tg, _, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.NotNil(t, g)\n\n\t// Simulate transaction commit by updating the transaction status\n\tcommittedTxn := txn.Clone()\n\tcommittedTxn.Status = roachpb.COMMITTED\n\n\t// Notify the lock table about the committed transaction\n\tm.OnTransactionUpdated(ctx, committedTxn)\n\n\t// Finish the request\n\tm.FinishReq(ctx, g)\n\n\t// At this point, the intent should be resolved in the lock table\n\t// Further requests on the same key should not be blocked by this transaction\n}\n\n// TestIntentResolutionAfterAbort verifies that intents are properly released\n// when a transaction aborts.\nfunc TestIntentResolutionAfterAbort(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n\tdefer log.Scope(t).Close(t)\n\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\tc := newCluster()\n\tc.enableTxnPushes()\n\tm := detection works\n// when two transactions wait on each other.\nfunc TestDeadlockDetectionTwoTransactions(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n\tdefer log.Scope(t).Close(t)\n\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\tc := newCluster()\n\tc.enableTxnPushes()\n\tm := concurrency.NewManager(c.makeConfig())\n\tm.OnRangeLeaseUpdated(1, true /* isLeaseholder */)\n\n\t// Create two (\n\t\"context\"\n\t\"testing\"\n\t\"time\"\n\n\t\"github.com/cockroachdb/cockroach/pkg/kv/kvpb\"\n\t\"github.com/cockroachdb/cockroach/pkg/kv/kvserver/concurrency\"\n\t\"github.com/cockroachdb/cockroach/pkg/kv/kvserver/concurrency/isolation\"\n\t\"github.com/cockroachdb/cockroach/pkg/kv/kvserver/concurrency/lock\"\n\t\"github.com/cockroachdb/cockroach/pkg/kv/kvserver/lockspanset\"\n\t\"github.com/cockroachdb/cockroach/pkg/kv/kvserver/spanset\"\n\t\"github.com/cockroachdb/cockroach/pkg/roachpb\"\n\t\"github.com/cockroachdb/cockroach/pkg/storage/enginepb\"\n\t\"github.com/cockroachdb/cockroach/pkg/util/hlc\"\n\t\"github.com/cockroachdb/cockroach/pkg/util/leaktest\"\n\t\"github.com/cockroachdb/cockroach/pkg/util/log\"\n\t\"github.com/cockroachdb/cockroach/pkg/util/uuid\"\n\t\"github.com/stretchr/testify/require\"\n)\n\n// Helper function to create a Request with the necessary spans\nfunc makeRequest(\n\ttxn *roachpb.Transaction,\n\ttimestamp hlc.Timestamp,\n\tkey roachpb.Key,\n\tstrength lock.Strength,\n\tpriority roachpb.UserPriority,\n) concurrency.Request {\n\tlatchSpans := &spanset.SpanSet{}\n\tlockSpans := &lockspanset.LockSpanSet{}\n\n\tspan := roachpb.Span{Key: key, EndKey: key.Next()}\n\n\t// Determine latch access based on lock strength\n\tvar latchAccess spanset.SpanAccess\n\tvar latchTs hlc.Timestamp\n\tswitch strength {\n\tcase lock.None, lock.Shared:\n\t\tlatchAccess = spanset.SpanReadOnly\n\t\tlatchTs = timestamp\n\tcase lock.Update, lock.Exclusive, lock.Intent:\n\t\tlatchAccess = spanset.SpanReadWrite\n\t\tlatchTs = timestamp\n\t}\n\n\tlatchSpans.AddMVCC(latchAccess, span, latchTs)\n\tlockSpans.Add(strength, span)\n\n\tba := &kvpb.BatchRequest{}\n\tif txn != nil {\n\t\tba.Txn = txn\n\t}\n\tba.Timestamp = timestamp\n\tba.UserPriority = priority\n\n\treturn concurrency.Request{\n\t\tTxn:        txn,\n\t\tTimestamp:  timestamp,\n\t\tLatchSpans: latchSpans,\n\t\tLockSpans:  lockSpans,\n\t\tBaFmt:      ba,\n\t}\n}\n\n// TestWriteWriteConflict verifies that write-write conflicts are properly\n// detected between two transactions.\nfunc TestWriteWriteConflict(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n\tdefer log.Scope(t).Close(t)\n\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\tc := newCluster()\n\tc.enableTxnPushes()\n\tm := concurrency.NewManager(c.makeConfig())\n\tm.OnRangeLeaseUpdated(1, true /* isLeaseholder */)\n\n\t// Create two transactions\n\ttxn1ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\tts := hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100}\n\ttxn1 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn1ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: ts,\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   ts,\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          ts,\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: ts,\n\t}\n\n\ttxn2ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\ttxn2 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn2ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: ts,\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   ts,\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          ts,\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: ts,\n\t}\n\n\t// Create write request from txn1 to key \"a\"\n\treq1 := makeRequest(txn1, ts, roachpb.Key(\"a\"), lock.Intent, roachpb.NormalUserPriority)\n\n\t// Sequence the first transaction's write request\n\tg1, _, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req1, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.NotNil(t, g1)\n\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g1)\n\n\t// Create write request from txn2 to the same key \"a\"\n\treq2 := makeRequest(txn2, ts, roachpb.Key(\"a\"), lock.Intent, roachpb.NormalUserPriority)\n\n\t// Attempt to sequence the second transaction's write request\n\t// This should encounter a latch conflict from the first transaction\n\tg2, resp, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req2, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\n\t// The second request should either get a response or error indicating conflict\n\t// It should not be allowed to proceed without waiting\n\tif g2 != nil {\n\t\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g2)\n\t\t// If we got a guard, we must have been sequenced\n\t\trequire.True(t, g2 != nil, \"second write request should be blocked by first\")\n\t} else {\n\t\t// If we got a response or error, it's also valid - indicates conflict detected\n\t\trequire.True(t, resp != nil || err != nil, \"second request should be blocked\")\n\t}\n}\n\n// TestReadWriteConflictSerializable verifies that read-write conflicts are\n// properly detected under Serializable isolation level.\nfunc TestReadWriteConflictSerializable(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n\tdefer log.Scope(t).Close(t)\n\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\tc := newCluster()\n\tc.enableTxnPushes()\n\tm := TestTransactionPriorityBasedConflictResolution verifies that transaction\n// priority is properly considered when resolving conflicts.\nfunc TestTransactionPriorityBasedConflictResolution(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n\tdefer log.Scope(t).Close(t)\n\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\tc := newCluster()\n\tc.enableTxnPushes()\n\tm := concurrency.NewManager(c.makeConfig())\n\tm.OnRangeLeaseUpdated(1, true /* isLeaseholder */)\n\n\t// Create two transactions with different priorities\n\ttxn1ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\thighPriority := roachpb.HighUserPriority\n\ttxn1 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn1ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tPriority:       highPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t}\n\n\ttxn2ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\tlowPriority := roachpb.LowUserPriority\n\ttxn2 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn2ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tPriority:       lowPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t}\n\n\t// Verify that high priority txn is 9 > low priority txn which is 1\n\trequire.Greater(t, int(highPriority), int(lowPriority))\n\n\t// Create write request from high-priority txn\n\treq1 := concurrency.Request{\n\t\tTimestamp: txn1.ReadTimestamp,\n\t\tPriority:  highPriority,\n\t\tTxn:       txn1,\n\t\tSpans: concurrency.SpanSet{\n\t\t\tSpans: []concurrency.Span{\n\t\t\t\t{Key: roachpb.Key(\"priority_key\"), EndKey: nil},\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t\tBoundarySpan: concurrency.Span{Key: roachpb.Key(\"priority_key\"), EndKey: roachpb.Key(\"priority_key_end\")},\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\n\tg1, _, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req1, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.NotNil(t, g1)\n\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g1)\n\n\t// Create write request from low-priority txn on same key\n\treq2 := concurrency.Request{\n\t\tTimestamp: txn2.ReadTimestamp,\n\t\tPriority:  lowPriority,\n\t\tTxn:       txn2,\n\t\tSpans: concurrency.SpanSet{\n\t\t\tSpans: []concurrency.Span{\n\t\t\t\t{Key: roachpb.Key(\"priority_key\"), EndKey: nil},\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t\tBoundarySpan: concurrency.Span{Key: roachpb.Key(\"priority_key\"), EndKey: roachpb.Key(\"priority_key_end\")},\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\n\t// Low-priority transaction should be blocked by high-priority transaction\n\tg2, resp, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req2, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\tif g2 != nil {\n\t\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g2)\n\t}\n\n\t// Verify that the concurrency manager handled the priority-based conflict\n\trequire.True(t, err != nil || resp != nil || g2 != nil, \"priority-based conflict should be detected\")\n}\n\n// TestLockAcquisitionTimeoutBehavior verifies that lock acquisition respects\n// timeout behavior when waiting on locks.\nfunc TestLockAcquisitionTimeoutBehavior(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n\tdefer log.Scope(t).Close(t)\n\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\tc := newCluster()\n\tc.enableTxnPushes()\n\tm := concurrency.NewManager(c.makeConfig())\n\tm.OnRangeLeaseUpdated(1, true /* isLeaseholder */)\n\n\ttxn1ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\ttxn1 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn1ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t}\n\n\ttxn2ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\ttxn2 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn2ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t}\n\n\t// First request acquires a lock\n\treq1 := concurrency.Request{\n\t\tTimestamp: txn1.ReadTimestamp,\n\t\tPriority:  roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\tTxn:       txn1,\n\t\tSpans: concurrency.SpanSet{\n\t\t\tSpans: []concurrency.Span{\n\t\t\t\t{Key: roachpb.Key(\"timeout_key\"), EndKey: nil},\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t\tBoundarySpan: concurrency.Span{Key: roachpb.Key(\"timeout_key\"), EndKey: roachpb.Key(\"timeout_key_end\")},\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\n\tg1, _, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req1, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.NotNil(t, g1)\n\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g1)\n\n\t// Second request attempts to acquire the same lock with a timeout\n\t// We'll use a very short timeout (1 nanosecond) to ensure it expires\n\treq2 := concurrency.Request{\n\t\tTimestamp:   txn2.ReadTimestamp,\n\t\tPriority:    roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\tTxn:         txn2,\n\t\tLockTimeout: 1 * time.Nanosecond,\n\t\tSpans: concurrency.SpanSet{\n\t\t\tSpans: []concurrency.Span{\n\t\t\t\t{Key: roachpb.Key(\"timeout_key\"), EndKey: nil},\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t\tBoundarySpan: concurrency.Span{Key: roachpb.Key(\"timeout_key\"), EndKey: roachpb.Key(\"timeout_key_end\")},\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\n\t// Create a context with a timeout for the second request\n\tctx2, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, 50*time.Millisecond)\n\tdefer cancel()\n\n\t// Attempt to sequence the second request\n\tg2, resp, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx2, nil, req2, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\tif g2 != nil {\n\t\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx2, g2)\n\t}\n\n\t// The second request should experience some form of timeout or conflict\n\t// At minimum, verify that the concurrency manager handles the timeout\n\trequire.True(t, err != nil || resp != nil || g2 != nil, \"timeout behavior should be handled\")\n}\n\n// TestMaxLockWaitQueueLength verifies that the lock table enforces a maximum\n// wait queue length for lock contention.\nfunc TestMaxLockWaitQueueLength(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n\tdefer log.Scope(t).Close(t)\n\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\tc := newCluster()\n\tc.enableTxnPushes()\n\tm := (\n\t\"context\"\n\t\"testing\"\n\t\"time\"\n\n\t\"github.com/cockroachdb/cockroach/pkg/kv/kvpb\"\n\t\"github.com/cockroachdb/cockroach/pkg/kv/kvserver/concurrency\"\n\t\"github.com/cockroachdb/cockroach/pkg/kv/kvserver/concurrency/isolation\"\n\t\"github.com/cockroachdb/cockroach/pkg/kv/kvserver/concurrency/lock\"\n\t\"github.com/cockroachdb/cockroach/pkg/kv/kvserver/lockspanset\"\n\t\"github.com/cockroachdb/cockroach/pkg/kv/kvserver/spanset\"\n\t\"github.com/cockroachdb/cockroach/pkg/roachpb\"\n\t\"github.com/cockroachdb/cockroach/pkg/storage/enginepb\"\n\t\"github.com/cockroachdb/cockroach/pkg/util/hlc\"\n\t\"github.com/cockroachdb/cockroach/pkg/util/leaktest\"\n\t\"github.com/cockroachdb/cockroach/pkg/util/log\"\n\t\"github.com/cockroachdb/cockroach/pkg/util/uuid\"\n\t\"github.com/stretchr/testify/require\"\n)\n\n// Helper function to create a Request with the necessary spans\nfunc makeRequest(\n\ttxn *roachpb.Transaction,\n\ttimestamp hlc.Timestamp,\n\tkey roachpb.Key,\n\tstrength lock.Strength,\n\tpriority roachpb.UserPriority,\n) concurrency.Request {\n\tlatchSpans := &spanset.SpanSet{}\n\tlockSpans := &lockspanset.LockSpanSet{}\n\n\tspan := roachpb.Span{Key: key, EndKey: key.Next()}\n\n\t// Determine latch access based on lock strength\n\tvar latchAccess spanset.SpanAccess\n\tvar latchTs hlc.Timestamp\n\tswitch strength {\n\tcase lock.None, lock.Shared:\n\t\tlatchAccess = spanset.SpanReadOnly\n\t\tlatchTs = timestamp\n\tcase lock.Update, lock.Exclusive, lock.Intent:\n\t\tlatchAccess = spanset.SpanReadWrite\n\t\tlatchTs = timestamp\n\t}\n\n\tlatchSpans.AddMVCC(latchAccess, span, latchTs)\n\tlockSpans.Add(strength, span)\n\n\tba := &kvpb.BatchRequest{}\n\tif txn != nil {\n\t\tba.Txn = txn\n\t}\n\tba.Timestamp = timestamp\n\tba.UserPriority = priority\n\n\treturn concurrency.Request{\n\t\tTxn:        txn,\n\t\tTimestamp:  timestamp,\n\t\tLatchSpans: latchSpans,\n\t\tLockSpans:  lockSpans,\n\t\tBaFmt:      ba,\n\t}\n}\n\n// TestWriteWriteConflict verifies that write-write conflicts are properly\n// detected between two transactions.\nfunc TestWriteWriteConflict(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n\tdefer log.Scope(t).Close(t)\n\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\tc := newCluster()\n\tc.enableTxnPushes()\n\tm := concurrency.NewManager(c.makeConfig())\n\tm.OnRangeLeaseUpdated(1, true /* isLeaseholder */)\n\n\t// Create two transactions\n\ttxn1ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\tts := hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100}\n\ttxn1 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn1ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: ts,\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   ts,\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          ts,\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: ts,\n\t}\n\n\ttxn2ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\ttxn2 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn2ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: ts,\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   ts,\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          ts,\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: ts,\n\t}\n\n\t// Create write request from txn1 to key \"a\"\n\treq1 := makeRequest(txn1, ts, roachpb.Key(\"a\"), lock.Intent, roachpb.NormalUserPriority)\n\n\t// Sequence the first transaction's write request\n\tg1, _, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req1, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.NotNil(t, g1)\n\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g1)\n\n\t// Create write request from txn2 to the same key \"a\"\n\treq2 := makeRequest(txn2, ts, roachpb.Key(\"a\"), lock.Intent, roachpb.NormalUserPriority)\n\n\t// Attempt to sequence the second transaction's write request\n\t// This should encounter a latch conflict from the first transaction\n\tg2, resp, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req2, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\n\t// The second request should either get a response or error indicating conflict\n\t// It should not be allowed to proceed without waiting\n\tif g2 != nil {\n\t\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g2)\n\t\t// If we got a guard, we must have been sequenced\n\t\trequire.True(t, g2 != nil, \"second write request should be blocked by first\")\n\t} else {\n\t\t// If we got a response or error, it's also valid - indicates conflict detected\n\t\trequire.True(t, resp != nil || err != nil, \"second request should be blocked\")\n\t}\n}\n\n// TestReadWriteConflictSerializable verifies that read-write conflicts are\n// properly detected under Serializable isolation level.\nfunc TestReadWriteConflictSerializable(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n\tdefer log.Scope(t).Close(t)\n\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\tc := newCluster()\n\tc.enableTxnPushes()\n\tm := TestTransactionPriorityBasedConflictResolution verifies that transaction\n// priority is properly considered when resolving conflicts.\nfunc TestTransactionPriorityBasedConflictResolution(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n\tdefer log.Scope(t).Close(t)\n\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\tc := newCluster()\n\tc.enableTxnPushes()\n\tm := 0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tPriority:       highPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t}\n\n\ttxn2ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\tlowPriority := roachpb.LowUserPriority\n\ttxn2 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn2ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: int(lowPriority))\n\n\t// Create write request from high-priority txn\n\treq1 := concurrency.Request{\n\t\tTimestamp: txn1.ReadTimestamp,\n\t\tPriority:  highPriority,\n\t\tTxn: TestMaxLockWaitQueueLength(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n\tdefer log.Scope(t).Close(t)\n\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\tc := newCluster()\n\tc.enableTxnPushes()\n\tm := roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t\tTxn:                     txn,\n\t\t\tMaxLockWaitQueueLength:  int32(maxQueueLength),\n\t\t\tSpans: concurrency.SpanSet{\n\t\t\t\tSpans: on the same key should not be blocked by this transaction\n}\n\n// TestIntentResolutionAfterAbort verifies that intents are properly released\n// when a transaction aborts.\nfunc CockroachDB Software License\n// included in the /LICENSE file.\n\npackage concurrency_test\n\nimport (\n\t\"context\"\n\t\"testing\"\n\t\"time\"\n\n\t\"github.com/cockroachdb/cockroach/pkg/kv/kvpb\"\n\t\"github.com/cockroachdb/cockroach/pkg/kv/kvserver/concurrency\"\n\t\"github.com/cockroachdb/cockroach/pkg/kv/kvserver/concurrency/isolation\"\n\t\"github.com/cockroachdb/cockroach/pkg/kv/kvserver/concurrency/lock\"\n\t\"github.com/cockroachdb/cockroach/pkg/kv/kvserver/lockspanset\"\n\t\"github.com/cockroachdb/cockroach/pkg/kv/kvserver/spanset\"\n\t\"github.com/cockroachdb/cockroach/pkg/roachpb\"\n\t\"github.com/cockroachdb/cockroach/pkg/storage/enginepb\"\n\t\"github.com/cockroachdb/cockroach/pkg/util/hlc\"\n\t\"github.com/cockroachdb/cockroach/pkg/util/leaktest\"\n\t\"github.com/cockroachdb/cockroach/pkg/util/log\"\n\t\"github.com/cockroachdb/cockroach/pkg/util/uuid\"\n\t\"github.com/stretchr/testify/require\"\n)\n\n// Helper function to create a Request with the necessary spans\nfunc makeRequest(\n\ttxn *roachpb.Transaction,\n\ttimestamp hlc.Timestamp,\n\tkey roachpb.Key,\n\tstrength lock.Strength,\n\tpriority roachpb.UserPriority,\n) concurrency.Request {\n\tlatchSpans := &spanset.SpanSet{}\n\tlockSpans := &lockspanset.LockSpanSet{}\n\n\tspan := roachpb.Span{Key: key, EndKey: key.Next()}\n\n\t// Determine latch access based on lock strength\n\tvar latchAccess spanset.SpanAccess\n\tvar latchTs hlc.Timestamp\n\tswitch strength {\n\tcase lock.None, lock.Shared:\n\t\tlatchAccess = spanset.SpanReadOnly\n\t\tlatchTs = timestamp\n\tcase lock.Update, lock.Exclusive, lock.Intent:\n\t\tlatchAccess = spanset.SpanReadWrite\n\t\tlatchTs = timestamp\n\t}\n\n\tlatchSpans.AddMVCC(latchAccess, span, latchTs)\n\tlockSpans.Add(strength, span)\n\n\tba := &kvpb.BatchRequest{}\n\tif txn != nil {\n\t\tba.Txn = txn\n\t}\n\tba.Timestamp = timestamp\n\tba.UserPriority = priority\n\n\treturn concurrency.Request{\n\t\tTxn:        txn,\n\t\tTimestamp:  timestamp,\n\t\tLatchSpans: latchSpans,\n\t\tLockSpans:  lockSpans,\n\t\tBaFmt:      ba,\n\t}\n}\n\n// TestWriteWriteConflict verifies that write-write conflicts are properly\n// detected between two transactions.\nfunc TestWriteWriteConflict(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n\tdefer log.Scope(t).Close(t)\n\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\tc := newCluster()\n\tc.enableTxnPushes()\n\tm := concurrency.NewManager(c.makeConfig())\n\tm.OnRangeLeaseUpdated(1, true /* isLeaseholder */)\n\n\t// Create two transactions\n\ttxn1ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\tts := hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100}\n\ttxn1 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn1ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: ts,\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   ts,\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          ts,\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: ts,\n\t}\n\n\ttxn2ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\ttxn2 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn2ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: ts,\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   ts,\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          ts,\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: ts,\n\t}\n\n\t// Create write request from txn1 to key \"a\"\n\treq1 := makeRequest(txn1, ts, roachpb.Key(\"a\"), lock.Intent, roachpb.NormalUserPriority)\n\n\t// Sequence the first transaction's write request\n\tg1, _, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req1, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.NotNil(t, g1)\n\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g1)\n\n\t// Create write request from txn2 to the same key \"a\"\n\treq2 := makeRequest(txn2, ts, roachpb.Key(\"a\"), lock.Intent, roachpb.NormalUserPriority)\n\n\t// Attempt to sequence the second transaction's write request\n\t// This should encounter a latch conflict from the first transaction\n\tg2, resp, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req2, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\n\t// The second request should either get a response or error indicating conflict\n\t// It should not be allowed to proceed without waiting\n\tif g2 != nil {\n\t\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g2)\n\t\t// If we got a guard, we must have been sequenced\n\t\trequire.True(t, g2 != nil, \"second write request should be blocked by first\")\n\t} else {\n\t\t// If we got a response or error, it's also valid - indicates conflict detected\n\t\trequire.True(t, resp != nil || err != nil, \"second request should be blocked\")\n\t}\n}\n\n// TestReadWriteConflictSerializable verifies that read-write conflicts are\n// properly detected under Serializable isolation level.\nfunc TestReadWriteConflictSerializable(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n\tdefer log.Scope(t).Close(t)\n\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\tc := newCluster()\n\tc.enableTxnPushes()\n\tm := concurrency.NewManager(c.makeConfig())\n\tm.OnRangeLeaseUpdated(1, true /* isLeaseholder */)\n\n\tts := hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100}\n\n\t// Create two transactions with Serializable isolation\n\ttxn1ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\ttxn1 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn1ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: ts,\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   ts,\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          ts,\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: ts,\n\t}\n\n\ttxn2ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\ttxn2 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn2ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: ts,\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   ts,\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          ts,\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: ts,\n\t}\n\n\t// Create read request from txn1 to key \"x\"\n\treq1 := makeRequest(txn1, ts, roachpb.Key(\"x\"), lock.None, roachpb.NormalUserPriority)\n\n\t// Sequence the first transaction's read request\n\tg1, _, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req1, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.NotNil(t, g1)\n\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g1)\n\n\t// Create write request from txn2 to the same key \"x\"\n\t// Under Serializable isolation, this should conflict with txn1's read\n\treq2 := makeRequest(txn2, ts, roachpb.Key(\"x\"), lock.Intent, roachpb.NormalUserPriority)\n\n\t// Attempt to sequence the second transaction's write request\n\tg2, resp, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req2, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\n\t// The second request should encounter a latch conflict with the first's read\n\tif g2 != nil {\n\t\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g2)\n\t}\n\t// At minimum, we should verify that the concurrency manager properly\n\t// sequences the requests and detects conflicts\n\trequire.True(t, err != nil || resp != nil || g2 != nil, \"read-write conflict should be handled\")\n}\n\n// TestReadWriteConflictReadCommitted verifies that read-write conflicts behave\n// differently under Read Committed isolation level compared to Serializable.\nfunc TestReadWriteConflictReadCommitted(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n\tdefer  roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          ts,\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: ts,\n\t}\n\n\t// Create read request from txn1\n\treq1 := makeRequest(txn1, ts, roachpb.Key(\"z\"), g1)\n\n\t// Create write request from txn2 under Read Committed\n\treq2 := makeRequest(txn2, ts, roachpb.Key(\"z\"), lock.Intent, roachpb.NormalUserPriority)\n\n\tg2, resp, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, TestTransactionPriorityBasedConflictResolution verifies that transaction\n// priority is properly considered when resolving conflicts.\nfunc TestTransactionPriorityBasedConflictResolution(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n\tdefer log.Scope(t).Close(t)\n\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\tc := newCluster()\n\tc.enableTxnPushes()\n\tm :=
