roachpb.HighUserPriority\n\ttxn1 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn1ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tPriority:       highPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t}\n\n\ttxn2ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\tlowPriority := roachpb.LowUserPriority\n\ttxn2 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn2ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: 100},\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t}\n\n\t// Verify that high priority txn is 9 > low priority txn which is 1\n\trequire.Greater(t, int(highPriority), int(lowPriority))\n\n\t// Create write request from high-priority txn\n\treq1 := concurrency.Request{\n\t\tTimestamp: txn1.ReadTimestamp,\n\t\tPriority:  highPriority,\n\t\tTxn:       txn1,\n\t\tSpans: concurrency.SpanSet{\n\t\t\tSpans: []concurrency.Span{\n\t\t\t\t{Key: roachpb.Key(\"priority_key\"), EndKey: nil},\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t\tBoundarySpan: concurrency.Span{Key: roachpb.Key(\"priority_key\"), EndKey: roachpb.Key(\"priority_key_end\")},\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\n\tg1, _, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req1, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.NotNil(t, g1)\n\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g1)\n\n\t// Create write request from low-priority txn on same key\n\treq2 := concurrency.Request{\n\t\tTimestamp: txn2.ReadTimestamp,\n\t\tPriority:  lowPriority,\n\t\tTxn:       txn2,\n\t\tSpans: concurrency.SpanSet{\n\t\t\tSpans: []concurrency.Span{\n\t\t\t\t{Key: roachpb.Key(\"priority_key\"), EndKey: nil},\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t\tBoundarySpan: concurrency.Span{Key: roachpb.Key(\"priority_key\"), EndKey: roachpb.Key(\"priority_key_end\")},\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\n\t// Low-priority transaction should be blocked by high-priority transaction\n\tg2, resp, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req2, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\tif g2 != nil {\n\t\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g2)\n\t}\n\n\t// Verify that the concurrency manager handled the priority-based conflict\n\trequire.True(t, err != nil || resp != nil || g2 != nil, \"priority-based conflict should be detected\")\n}\n\n// TestLockAcquisitionTimeoutBehavior verifies that lock acquisition respects\n// timeout behavior when waiting on locks.\nfunc TestLockAcquisitionTimeoutBehavior(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n\tdefer log.Scope(t).Close(t)\n\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\tc := newCluster()\n\tc.enableTxnPushes()\n\tm := concurrency.NewManager(c.makeConfig())\n\tm.OnRangeLeaseUpdated(1, true /* isLeaseholder */)\n\n\ttxn1ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\ttxn1 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn1ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t}\n\n\ttxn2ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\ttxn2 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn2ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t}\n\n\t// First request acquires a lock\n\treq1 := concurrency.Request{\n\t\tTimestamp: txn1.ReadTimestamp,\n\t\tPriority:  roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\tTxn:       txn1,\n\t\tSpans: concurrency.SpanSet{\n\t\t\tSpans: []concurrency.Span{\n\t\t\t\t{Key: roachpb.Key(\"timeout_key\"), EndKey: nil},\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t\tBoundarySpan: concurrency.Span{Key: roachpb.Key(\"timeout_key\"), EndKey: roachpb.Key(\"timeout_key_end\")},\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\n\tg1, _, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req1, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.NotNil(t, g1)\n\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g1)\n\n\t// Second request attempts to acquire the same lock with a timeout\n\t// We'll use a very short timeout (1 nanosecond) to ensure it expires\n\treq2 := concurrency.Request{\n\t\tTimestamp:   txn2.ReadTimestamp,\n\t\tPriority:    roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\tTxn:         txn2,\n\t\tLockTimeout: 1 * time.Nanosecond,\n\t\tSpans: EndKey: roachpb.Key(\"timeout_key_end\")},\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\n\t// Create a context with a timeout for the second request\n\tctx2, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, 50*time.Millisecond)\n\tdefer cancel()\n\n\t// Attempt to sequence the second request\n\tg2, resp, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx2, nil, req2, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\tif g2 != nil {\n\t\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx2, g2)\n\t}\n\n\t// The second request should experience some form of timeout or conflict\n\t// At minimum, verify that the concurrency manager handles the timeout\n\trequire.True(t, err != nil || resp != nil || g2 != nil, \"timeout behavior should be handled\")\n}\n\n// TestMaxLockWaitQueueLength verifies that the lock table enforces a maximum\n// wait queue length for lock contention.\nfunc TestMaxLockWaitQueueLength(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n\tdefer log.Scope(t).Close(t)\n\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\tc := newCluster()\n\tc.enableTxnPushes()\n\tm := concurrency.NewManager(c.makeConfig())\n\tm.OnRangeLeaseUpdated(1, true /* isLeaseholder */)\n\n\t// Create a first transaction that will hold a lock\n\ttxn1ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\ttxn1 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn1ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t}\n\n\t// First request holds a lock\n\treq1 := concurrency.Request{\n\t\tTimestamp: txn1.ReadTimestamp,\n\t\tPriority:  roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\tTxn:       txn1,\n\t\tSpans: concurrency.SpanSet{\n\t\t\tSpans: []concurrency.Span{\n\t\t\t\t{Key: roachpb.Key(\"queue_key\"), EndKey: nil},\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t\tBoundarySpan: concurrency.Span{Key: roachpb.Key(\"queue_key\"), EndKey: roachpb.Key(\"queue_key_end\")},\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\n\tg1, _, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req1, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.NotNil(t, g1)\n\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g1)\n\n\t// Create multiple transactions trying to acquire the same lock\n\t// to test the wait queue length enforcement\n\tmaxQueueLength := 2\n\tfor i := 0; i < maxQueueLength+2; i++ {\n\t\ttxnID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\t\ttxn := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\t\tID:             txnID,\n\t\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t\tReadTimestamp:          hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t}\n\n\t\treq := concurrency.Request{\n\t\t\tTimestamp:               txn.ReadTimestamp,\n\t\t\tPriority:                roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t\tTxn:                     txn,\n\t\t\tMaxLockWaitQueueLength:  int32(maxQueueLength),\n\t\t\tSpans: concurrency.SpanSet{\n\t\t\t\tSpans: []concurrency.Span{\n\t\t\t\t\t{Key: roachpb.Key(\"queue_key\"), EndKey: nil},\n\t\t\t\t},\n\t\t\t\tBoundarySpan: concurrency.Span{Key: roachpb.Key(\"queue_key\"), EndKey: roachpb.Key(\"queue_key_end\")},\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tg, _, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\t\tif g != nil {\n\t\t\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// After exceeding max queue length, subsequent requests should fail\n\t\tif i >= maxQueueLength {\n\t\t\trequire.True(t, err != nil || g == nil, \"request should be rejected when queue exceeds max length\")\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}\n\n// TestIntentResolutionAfterCommit verifies that intents are properly resolved\n// when a transaction commits.\nfunc TestIntentResolutionAfterCommit(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n\tdefer log.Scope(t).Close(t)\n\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\tc := newCluster()\n\tc.enableTxnPushes()\n\tm := concurrency.NewManager(c.makeConfig())\n\tm.OnRangeLeaseUpdated(1, true /* isLeaseholder */)\n\n\ttxnID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\ttxn := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txnID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t}\n\n\t// concurrency.SpanSet{\n\t\t\tSpans: []concurrency.Span{\n\t\t\t\t{Key: roachpb.Key(\"intent_key\"), EndKey: nil},\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t\tBoundarySpan: concurrency.Span{Key: roachpb.Key(\"intent_key\"), EndKey: roachpb.Key(\"intent_key_end\")},\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\n\tg, _, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.NotNil(t, g)\n\n\t// Simulate transaction commit by updating the transaction status\n\tcommittedTxn := txn.Clone()\n\tcommittedTxn.Status = roachpb.COMMITTED\n\n\t// Notify the lock table about the committed transaction\n\tm.OnTransactionUpdated(ctx, committedTxn)\n\n\t// Finish the request\n\tm.FinishReq(ctx, g)\n\n\t// At this point, the intent should be resolved in the lock table\n\t// Further requests on the same key should not be blocked by this transaction\n}\n\n// TestIntentResolutionAfterAbort verifies that intents are properly released\n// when a transaction aborts.\nfunc detection works\n// when two transactions wait on each other.\nfunc TestDeadlockDetectionTwoTransactions(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n\tdefer log.Scope(t).Close(t)\n\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\tc := newCluster()\n\tc.enableTxnPushes()\n\tm := concurrency.NewManager(c.makeConfig())\n\tm.OnRangeLeaseUpdated(1, true /* isLeaseholder */)\n\n\t// Create two transactions\n\ttxn1ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\ttxn1 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn1ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t}\n\n\ttxn2ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\ttxn2 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn2ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t}\n\n\t// txn1 writes to key \"a\"\n\treq1 := concurrency.Request{\n\t\tTimestamp: txn1.ReadTimestamp,\n\t\tPriority:  roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\tTxn:       txn1,\n\t\tSpans: concurrency.SpanSet{\n\t\t\tSpans: []concurrency.Span{\n\t\t\t\t{Key: roachpb.Key(\"deadlock_a\"), EndKey: nil},\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t\tBoundarySpan: concurrency.Span{Key: roachpb.Key(\"deadlock_a\"), concurrency.SpanSet{\n\t\t\tSpans: []concurrency.Span{\n\t\t\t\t{Key: roachpb.Key(\"deadlock_b\"), EndKey: nil},\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t\tBoundarySpan: concurrency.Span{Key: roachpb.Key(\"deadlock_b\"), EndKey: roachpb.Key(\"deadlock_b_end\")},\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\n\tg2, _, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req2, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.NotNil(t, g2)\n\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g2)\n\n\t// txn1 tries to write to key \"b\" (already held by txn2)\n\t// This creates a potential deadlock scenario\n\treq1b := concurrency.Request{\n\t\tTimestamp: txn1.ReadTimestamp,\n\t\tPriority:  roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\tTxn:       txn1,\n\t\tSpans: concurrency.SpanSet{\n\t\t\tSpans: []concurrency.Span{\n\t\t\t\t{Key: roachpb.Key(\"deadlock_b\"), EndKey: nil},\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t\tBoundarySpan: concurrency.Span{Key: roachpb.Key(\"deadlock_b\"), EndKey: roachpb.Key(\"deadlock_b_end\")},\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\n\tg1b, resp1b, err1b := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req1b, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\tif g1b != nil {\n\t\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g1b)\n\t}\n\n\t// The system should detect or handle the potential deadlock scenario\n\t// At minimum, verify that the sequencing completes without panic\n\trequire.True(t, err1b != nil || resp1b != nil || g1b != nil, \"deadlock scenario should be handled\")\n}\n\n// TestConcurrentRequestsOnDifferentKeys log.Scope(t).Close(t)\n\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\tc := newCluster()\n\tc.enableTxnPushes()\n\tm := concurrency.NewManager(c.makeConfig())\n\tm.OnRangeLeaseUpdated(1, true /* isLeaseholder */)\n\n\t// Create two transactions\n\ttxn1ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\ttxn1 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn1ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel: Helper function to create a Request with the necessary spans\nfunc makeRequest(\n\ttxn *roachpb.Transaction,\n\ttimestamp hlc.Timestamp,\n\tkey roachpb.Key,\n\tstrength lock.Strength,\n\tpriority roachpb.UserPriority,\n) concurrency.Request {\n\tlatchSpans := &spanset.SpanSet{}\n\tlockSpans := &lockspanset.LockSpanSet{}\n\n\tspan := roachpb.Span{Key: key, EndKey: key.Next()}\n\n\t// Determine latch access based on lock strength\n\tvar latchAccess spanset.SpanAccess\n\tvar latchTs hlc.Timestamp\n\tswitch strength {\n\tcase lock.None, lock.Shared:\n\t\tlatchAccess = spanset.SpanReadOnly\n\t\tlatchTs = timestamp\n\tcase lock.Update, lock.Exclusive, lock.Intent:\n\t\tlatchAccess = spanset.SpanReadWrite\n\t\tlatchTs = timestamp\n\t}\n\n\tlatchSpans.AddMVCC(latchAccess, span, latchTs)\n\tlockSpans.Add(strength, span)\n\n\tba := &kvpb.BatchRequest{}\n\tif txn != nil {\n\t\tba.Txn = txn\n\t}\n\tba.Timestamp = timestamp\n\tba.UserPriority = priority\n\n\treturn concurrency.Request{\n\t\tTxn:        txn,\n\t\tTimestamp:  timestamp,\n\t\tLatchSpans: latchSpans,\n\t\tLockSpans:  lockSpans,\n\t\tBaFmt:      ba,\n\t}\n}\n\n// TestWriteWriteConflict verifies that write-write conflicts are properly\n// detected between two transactions.\nfunc TestWriteWriteConflict(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n\tdefer log.Scope(t).Close(t)\n\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\tc := newCluster()\n\tc.enableTxnPushes()\n\tm := concurrency.NewManager(c.makeConfig())\n\tm.OnRangeLeaseUpdated(1, true /* isLeaseholder */)\n\n\t// Create two transactions\n\ttxn1ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\tts := hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100}\n\ttxn1 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn1ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: ts,\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   ts,\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          ts,\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: ts,\n\t}\n\n\ttxn2ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\ttxn2 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn2ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: ts,\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   ts,\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          ts,\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: ts,\n\t}\n\n\t// Create write request from txn1 to key \"a\"\n\treq1 := makeRequest(txn1, ts, roachpb.Key(\"a\"), lock.Intent, roachpb.NormalUserPriority)\n\n\t// Sequence the first transaction's write request\n\tg1, _, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req1, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.NotNil(t, g1)\n\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g1)\n\n\t// Create write request from txn2 to the same key \"a\"\n\treq2 := makeRequest(txn2, ts, roachpb.Key(\"a\"), lock.Intent, roachpb.NormalUserPriority)\n\n\t// Attempt to sequence the second transaction's write request\n\t// This should encounter a latch conflict from the first transaction\n\tg2, resp, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req2, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\n\t// The second request should either get a response or error indicating conflict\n\t// It should not be allowed to proceed without waiting\n\tif g2 != nil {\n\t\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g2)\n\t\t// If we got a guard, we must have been sequenced\n\t\trequire.True(t, g2 != nil, \"second write request should be blocked by first\")\n\t} else {\n\t\t// If we got a response or error, it's also valid - indicates conflict detected\n\t\trequire.True(t, resp != nil || err != nil, \"second request should be blocked\")\n\t}\n}\n\n// TestReadWriteConflictSerializable verifies that read-write conflicts are\n// properly detected under Serializable isolation level.\nfunc TestMaxLockWaitQueueLength(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n\tdefer log.Scope(t).Close(t)\n\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\tc := newCluster()\n\tc.enableTxnPushes()\n\tm := 100},\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t}\n\n\t// First request holds a lock\n\treq1 := concurrency.Request{\n\t\tTimestamp: txn1.ReadTimestamp,\n\t\tPriority:  roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\tTxn:       txn1,\n\t\tSpans: concurrency.SpanSet{\n\t\t\tSpans: []concurrency.Span{\n\t\t\t\t{Key: roachpb.Key(\"queue_key\"), EndKey: wait queue length enforcement\n\tmaxQueueLength := 2\n\tfor i := 0; i < maxQueueLength+2; i++ {\n\t\ttxnID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\t\ttxn := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\t\tTxnMeta: 100},\n\t\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t}\n\n\t\treq := concurrency.Request{\n\t\t\tTimestamp:               txn.ReadTimestamp,\n\t\t\tPriority:                roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t\tTxn:                     txn,\n\t\t\tMaxLockWaitQueueLength:  int32(maxQueueLength),\n\t\t\tSpans: concurrency.SpanSet{\n\t\t\t\tSpans: []concurrency.Span{\n\t\t\t\t\t{Key: roachpb.Key(\"queue_key\"), EndKey: nil},\n\t\t\t\t},\n\t\t\t\tBoundarySpan: concurrency.Span{Key: roachpb.Key(\"queue_key\"), EndKey: roachpb.Key(\"queue_key_end\")},\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tg, _, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\t\tif g != nil {\n\t\t\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// After exceeding max queue length, subsequent requests should fail\n\t\tif i >= maxQueueLength {\n\t\t\trequire.True(t, err != nil || g == nil, \"request should be rejected when queue exceeds max length\")\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}\n\n// TestIntentResolutionAfterCommit verifies that intents are properly resolved\n// when a transaction commits.\nfunc TestIntentResolutionAfterCommit(t *testing.T) Helper function to create a Request with the necessary spans\nfunc makeRequest(\n\ttxn *roachpb.Transaction,\n\ttimestamp hlc.Timestamp,\n\tkey roachpb.Key,\n\tstrength lock.Strength,\n\tpriority roachpb.UserPriority,\n) concurrency.Request {\n\tlatchSpans := &spanset.SpanSet{}\n\tlockSpans := &lockspanset.LockSpanSet{}\n\n\tspan := roachpb.Span{Key: key, EndKey: key.Next()}\n\n\t// Determine latch access based on lock strength\n\tvar latchAccess spanset.SpanAccess\n\tvar latchTs hlc.Timestamp\n\tswitch strength {\n\tcase lock.None, lock.Shared:\n\t\tlatchAccess = spanset.SpanReadOnly\n\t\tlatchTs = timestamp\n\tcase lock.Update, lock.Exclusive, lock.Intent:\n\t\tlatchAccess = spanset.SpanReadWrite\n\t\tlatchTs = timestamp\n\t}\n\n\tlatchSpans.AddMVCC(latchAccess, span, latchTs)\n\tlockSpans.Add(strength, span)\n\n\tba := &kvpb.BatchRequest{}\n\tif txn != nil {\n\t\tba.Txn = txn\n\t}\n\tba.Timestamp = timestamp\n\tba.UserPriority = priority\n\n\treturn concurrency.Request{\n\t\tTxn:        txn,\n\t\tTimestamp:  timestamp,\n\t\tLatchSpans: latchSpans,\n\t\tLockSpans:  lockSpans,\n\t\tBaFmt:      ba,\n\t}\n}\n\n// TestWriteWriteConflict verifies that write-write conflicts are properly\n// detected between two transactions.\nfunc TestWriteWriteConflict(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n\tdefer log.Scope(t).Close(t)\n\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\tc := newCluster()\n\tc.enableTxnPushes()\n\tm := concurrency.NewManager(c.makeConfig())\n\tm.OnRangeLeaseUpdated(1, true /* isLeaseholder */)\n\n\t// Create two transactions\n\ttxn1ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\tts := hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100}\n\ttxn1 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn1ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: ts,\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   ts,\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          ts,\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: ts,\n\t}\n\n\ttxn2ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\ttxn2 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn2ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: ts,\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   ts,\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          ts,\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: ts,\n\t}\n\n\t// Create write request from txn1 to key \"a\"\n\treq1 := makeRequest(txn1, ts, roachpb.Key(\"a\"), lock.Intent, roachpb.NormalUserPriority)\n\n\t// Sequence the first transaction's write request\n\tg1, _, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req1, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.NotNil(t, g1)\n\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g1)\n\n\t// Create write request from txn2 to the same key \"a\"\n\treq2 := makeRequest(txn2, ts, roachpb.Key(\"a\"), lock.Intent, roachpb.NormalUserPriority)\n\n\t// Attempt to sequence the second transaction's write request\n\t// This should encounter a latch conflict from the first transaction\n\tg2, resp, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req2, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\n\t// The second request should either get a response or error indicating conflict\n\t// It should not be allowed to proceed without waiting\n\tif g2 != nil {\n\t\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g2)\n\t\t// If we got a guard, we must have been sequenced\n\t\trequire.True(t, g2 != nil, \"second write request should be blocked by first\")\n\t} else {\n\t\t// If we got a response or error, it's also valid - indicates conflict detected\n\t\trequire.True(t, resp != nil || err != nil, \"second request should be blocked\")\n\t}\n}\n\n// TestReadWriteConflictSerializable verifies that read-write conflicts are\n// properly detected under Serializable isolation level.\nfunc TestMaxLockWaitQueueLength(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n\tdefer log.Scope(t).Close(t)\n\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\tc := newCluster()\n\tc.enableTxnPushes()\n\tm := 0,\n\t\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t\tReadTimestamp:          hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t}\n\n\t\treq := concurrency.Request{\n\t\t\tTimestamp: concurrency.SpanSet{\n\t\t\t\tSpans: []concurrency.Span{\n\t\t\t\t\t{Key: roachpb.Key(\"queue_key\"), EndKey: nil},\n\t\t\t\t},\n\t\t\t\tBoundarySpan: concurrency.Span{Key: roachpb.Key(\"queue_key\"), EndKey: roachpb.Key(\"queue_key_end\")},\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tg, _, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\t\tif g != nil {\n\t\t\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// After exceeding max queue length, subsequent requests should fail\n\t\tif i >= maxQueueLength {\n\t\t\trequire.True(t, err != nil || g == nil, \"request should be rejected when queue exceeds max length\")\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}\n\n// TestIntentResolutionAfterCommit verifies that intents are properly resolved\n// when a transaction commits.\nfunc TestIntentResolutionAfterCommit(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n\tdefer log.Scope(t).Close(t)\n\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\tc := newCluster()\n\tc.enableTxnPushes()\n\tm := transaction commit by updating the transaction status\n\tcommittedTxn := txn.Clone()\n\tcommittedTxn.Status = roachpb.COMMITTED\n\n\t// Notify the lock table about the committed transaction\n\tm.OnTransactionUpdated(ctx, committedTxn)\n\n\t// Finish the request\n\tm.FinishReq(ctx, g)\n\n\t// At this point, the intent should be resolved in the lock table\n\t// Further requests on the same key should not be blocked by this transaction\n}\n\n// TestIntentResolutionAfterAbort verifies that intents are properly released\n// when a transaction aborts.\nfunc Helper function to create a Request with the necessary spans\nfunc makeRequest(\n\ttxn *roachpb.Transaction,\n\ttimestamp hlc.Timestamp,\n\tkey roachpb.Key,\n\tstrength lock.Strength,\n\tpriority roachpb.UserPriority,\n) concurrency.Request {\n\tlatchSpans := &spanset.SpanSet{}\n\tlockSpans := &lockspanset.LockSpanSet{}\n\n\tspan := roachpb.Span{Key: key, EndKey: key.Next()}\n\n\t// Determine latch access based on lock strength\n\tvar latchAccess spanset.SpanAccess\n\tvar latchTs hlc.Timestamp\n\tswitch strength {\n\tcase lock.None, lock.Shared:\n\t\tlatchAccess = spanset.SpanReadOnly\n\t\tlatchTs = timestamp\n\tcase lock.Update, lock.Exclusive, lock.Intent:\n\t\tlatchAccess = spanset.SpanReadWrite\n\t\tlatchTs = timestamp\n\t}\n\n\tlatchSpans.AddMVCC(latchAccess, span, latchTs)\n\tlockSpans.Add(strength, span)\n\n\tba := &kvpb.BatchRequest{}\n\tif txn != nil {\n\t\tba.Txn = txn\n\t}\n\tba.Timestamp = timestamp\n\tba.UserPriority = priority\n\n\treturn concurrency.Request{\n\t\tTxn:        txn,\n\t\tTimestamp:  timestamp,\n\t\tLatchSpans: latchSpans,\n\t\tLockSpans:  lockSpans,\n\t\tBaFmt:      ba,\n\t}\n}\n\n// TestWriteWriteConflict verifies that write-write conflicts are properly\n// detected between two transactions.\nfunc TestWriteWriteConflict(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n\tdefer log.Scope(t).Close(t)\n\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\tc := newCluster()\n\tc.enableTxnPushes()\n\tm := concurrency.NewManager(c.makeConfig())\n\tm.OnRangeLeaseUpdated(1, true /* isLeaseholder */)\n\n\t// Create two transactions\n\ttxn1ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\tts := hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100}\n\ttxn1 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn1ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: ts,\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   ts,\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          ts,\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: ts,\n\t}\n\n\ttxn2ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\ttxn2 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn2ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: ts,\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   ts,\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          ts,\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: ts,\n\t}\n\n\t// Create write request from txn1 to key \"a\"\n\treq1 := makeRequest(txn1, ts, roachpb.Key(\"a\"), lock.Intent, roachpb.NormalUserPriority)\n\n\t// Sequence the first transaction's write request\n\tg1, _, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req1, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.NotNil(t, g1)\n\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g1)\n\n\t// Create write request from txn2 to the same key \"a\"\n\treq2 := makeRequest(txn2, ts, roachpb.Key(\"a\"), lock.Intent, roachpb.NormalUserPriority)\n\n\t// Attempt to sequence the second transaction's write request\n\t// This should encounter a latch conflict from the first transaction\n\tg2, resp, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req2, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\n\t// The second request should either get a response or error indicating conflict\n\t// It should not be allowed to proceed without waiting\n\tif g2 != nil {\n\t\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g2)\n\t\t// If we got a guard, we must have been sequenced\n\t\trequire.True(t, g2 != nil, \"second write request should be blocked by first\")\n\t} else {\n\t\t// If we got a response or error, it's also valid - indicates conflict detected\n\t\trequire.True(t, resp != nil || err != nil, \"second request should be blocked\")\n\t}\n}\n\n// TestReadWriteConflictSerializable verifies that read-write conflicts are\n// properly detected under Serializable isolation level.\nfunc TestReadWriteConflictSerializable(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n\tdefer log.Scope(t).Close(t)\n\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\tc := newCluster()\n\tc.enableTxnPushes()\n\tm :=     0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: ts,\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   ts,\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          ts,\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: ts,\n\t}\n\n\ttxn2ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\ttxn2 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn2ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: ts,\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   ts,\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          ts,\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: ts,\n\t}\n\n\t// Create read request from txn1 to key \"x\"\n\treq1 := makeRequest(txn1, ts, roachpb.Key(\"x\"), lock.None, roachpb.NormalUserPriority)\n\n\t// Sequence the first transaction's read request\n\tg1, _, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req1, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.NotNil(t, g1)\n\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g1)\n\n\t// Create write request from txn2 to the same key \"x\"\n\t// Under Serializable isolation, this should conflict with txn1's read\n\treq2 := makeRequest(txn2, ts, roachpb.Key(\"x\"), lock.Intent, roachpb.NormalUserPriority)\n\n\t// Attempt to sequence the second transaction's write request\n\tg2, resp, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req2, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\n\t// The second request should encounter a latch conflict with the first's read\n\tif g2 != nil {\n\t\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g2)\n\t}\n\t// At minimum, we should verify that the concurrency manager properly\n\t// sequences the requests and detects conflicts\n\trequire.True(t, err != nil || resp != nil || g2 != nil, \"read-write conflict should be handled\")\n}\n\n// TestReadWriteConflictReadCommitted verifies that read-write conflicts TestTransactionPriorityBasedConflictResolution verifies that transaction\n// priority is properly considered when resolving conflicts.\nfunc TestTransactionPriorityBasedConflictResolution(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n\tdefer log.Scope(t).Close(t)\n\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\tc := newCluster()\n\tc.enableTxnPushes()\n\tm := concurrency.NewManager(c.makeConfig())\n\tm.OnRangeLeaseUpdated(1, true /* isLeaseholder */)\n\n\tts := hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100}\n\n\t// Create two transactions with different priorities\n\ttxn1ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\thighPriority := roachpb.HighUserPriority\n\ttxn1 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn1ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: ts,\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   ts,\n\t\t\tPriority:       highPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          ts,\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: ts,\n\t}\n\n\ttxn2ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\tlowPriority := roachpb.LowUserPriority\n\ttxn2 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn2ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: ts,\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   ts,\n\t\t\tPriority:       lowPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          ts,\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: ts,\n\t}\n\n\t// Verify that high priority txn is 9 > low priority txn which is 1\n\trequire.Greater(t, int(highPriority), int(lowPriority))\n\n\t// Create write request from high-priority txn\n\treq1 := makeRequest(txn1, ts, roachpb.Key(\"priority_key\"), lock.Intent, highPriority)\n\n\tg1, _, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req1, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.NotNil(t, g1)\n\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g1)\n\n\t// Create write request from low-priority txn on same key\n\treq2 := makeRequest(txn2, ts, roachpb.Key(\"priority_key\"), lock.Intent, lowPriority)\n\n\t// Low-priority transaction should be blocked by high-priority transaction\n\tg2, resp, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req2, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\tif g2 != nil {\n\t\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g2)\n\t}\n\n\t// Verify that the concurrency manager handled the priority-based conflict\n\trequire.True(t, err != nil || resp != nil || g2 != nil, \"priority-based conflict should be detected\")\n}\n\n// TestLockAcquisitionTimeoutBehavior verifies that lock acquisition respects\n// timeout behavior when waiting on locks.\nfunc TestLockAcquisitionTimeoutBehavior(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n\tdefer log.Scope(t).Close(t)\n\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\tc := newCluster()\n\tc.enableTxnPushes()\n\tm := concurrency.NewManager(c.makeConfig())\n\tm.OnRangeLeaseUpdated(1, true /* isLeaseholder */)\n\n\tts := hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100}\n\n\ttxn1ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\ttxn1 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn1ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: ts,\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   ts,\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          ts,\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: ts,\n\t}\n\n\ttxn2ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\ttxn2 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn2ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: ts,\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   ts,\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          ts,\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: ts,\n\t}\n\n\t// First request acquires a lock\n\treq1 := makeRequest(txn1, ts, roachpb.Key(\"timeout_key\"), lock.Intent, roachpb.NormalUserPriority)\n\n\tg1, _, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req1, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.NotNil(t, g1)\n\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g1)\n\n\t// Second request attempts to acquire the same lock with a timeout\n\t// We'll use a very short timeout (1 nanosecond) to ensure it expires\n\treq2 := makeRequest(txn2, ts, roachpb.Key(\"timeout_key\"), lock.Intent, roachpb.NormalUserPriority)\n\treq2.LockTimeout = 1 * time.Nanosecond\n\n\t// Create a context with a timeout for the second request\n\tctx2, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, 50*time.Millisecond)\n\tdefer cancel()\n\n\t// Attempt to sequence the second request\n\tg2, resp, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx2, nil, req2, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\tif g2 != nil {\n\t\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx2, g2)\n\t}\n\n\t// The second request should experience some form of timeout or conflict\n\t// At minimum, verify that the concurrency manager handles the timeout\n\trequire.True(t, err != nil || resp != nil || g2 != nil, \"timeout behavior should be handled\")\n}\n\n// TestMaxLockWaitQueueLength verifies that the lock table enforces a maximum\n// wait queue length for lock contention.\nfunc TestMaxLockWaitQueueLength(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n\tdefer log.Scope(t).Close(t)\n\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\tc := newCluster()\n\tc.enableTxnPushes()\n\tm := concurrency.NewManager(c.makeConfig())\n\tm.OnRangeLeaseUpdated(1, true /* isLeaseholder */)\n\n\t// Create a first transaction that will hold a lock\n\ttxn1ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\ttxn1 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn1ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t}\n\n\t// First request holds a lock\n\treq1 := makeRequest(txn1, ts, roachpb.Key(\"queue_key\"), lock.Intent, roachpb.NormalUserPriority)\n\n\tg1, _, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req1, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.NotNil(t, g1)\n\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g1)\n\n\t// Create multiple transactions trying to acquire the same lock\n\t// to test the wait queue length enforcement\n\tmaxQueueLength := 2\n\tfor i := 0; i < maxQueueLength+2; i++ {\n\t\ttxnID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\t\ttxn := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\t\tID:             txnID,\n\t\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t\tReadTimestamp:          hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t}\n\n\t\treq := concurrency.Request{\n\t\t\tTimestamp:               txn.ReadTimestamp,\n\t\t\tPriority:                roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t\tTxn:                     txn,\n\t\t\tMaxLockWaitQueueLength:  int32(maxQueueLength),\n\t\t\tSpans: concurrency.SpanSet{\n\t\t\t\tSpans: []concurrency.Span{\n\t\t\t\t\t{Key: roachpb.Key(\"queue_key\"), EndKey: nil},\n\t\t\t\t},\n\t\t\t\tBoundarySpan: concurrency.Span{Key: roachpb.Key(\"queue_key\"), EndKey: roachpb.Key(\"queue_key_end\")},\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tg, _, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\t\tif g != nil {\n\t\t\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// After exceeding max queue length, subsequent requests should fail\n\t\tif i >= maxQueueLength {\n\t\t\trequire.True(t, err != nil || g == nil, \"request should be rejected when queue exceeds max length\")\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}\n\n// TestIntentResolutionAfterCommit verifies that intents are properly resolved\n// when a transaction commits.\nfunc TestIntentResolutionAfterCommit(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n\tdefer log.Scope(t).Close(t)\n\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\tc := newCluster()\n\tc.enableTxnPushes()\n\tm := concurrency.NewManager(c.makeConfig())\n\tm.OnRangeLeaseUpdated(1, true /* isLeaseholder */)\n\n\ttxnID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\ttxn := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txnID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t}\n\n\t// Create a write request that will create an intent\n\treq := concurrency.Request{\n\t\tTimestamp: txn.ReadTimestamp,\n\t\tPriority:  roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\tTxn:       txn,\n\t\tSpans: concurrency.SpanSet{\n\t\t\tSpans: []concurrency.Span{\n\t\t\t\t{Key: roachpb.Key(\"intent_key\"), EndKey: nil},\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t\tBoundarySpan: concurrency.Span{Key: roachpb.Key(\"intent_key\"), EndKey: roachpb.Key(\"intent_key_end\")},\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\n\tg, _, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.NotNil(t, g)\n\n\t// Simulate transaction commit by updating the transaction status\n\tcommittedTxn := txn.Clone()\n\tcommittedTxn.Status = roachpb.COMMITTED\n\n\t// Notify the lock table about the committed transaction\n\tm.OnTransactionUpdated(ctx, committedTxn)\n\n\t// Finish the request\n\tm.FinishReq(ctx, g)\n\n\t// At this point, the intent should be resolved in the lock table\n\t// Further requests on the same key should not be blocked by this transaction\n}\n\n// TestIntentResolutionAfterAbort verifies that intents are properly released\n// when a transaction aborts.\nfunc TestIntentResolutionAfterAbort(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n\tdefer log.Scope(t).Close(t)\n\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\tc := newCluster()\n\tc.enableTxnPushes()\n\tm := concurrency.NewManager(c.makeConfig())\n\tm.OnRangeLeaseUpdated(1, true /* isLeaseholder */)\n\n\ttxnID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\ttxn := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txnID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t}\n\n\t// Create a write request\n\treq := concurrency.Request{\n\t\tTimestamp: txn.ReadTimestamp,\n\t\tPriority:  roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\tTxn:       txn,\n\t\tSpans: concurrency.SpanSet{\n\t\t\tSpans: []concurrency.Span{\n\t\t\t\t{Key: roachpb.Key(\"abort_key\"), EndKey: nil},\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t\tBoundarySpan: concurrency.Span{Key: roachpb.Key(\"abort_key\"), EndKey: roachpb.Key(\"abort_key_end\")},\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\n\tg, _, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.NotNil(t, g)\n\n\t// Simulate roachpb.UserPriority,\n) concurrency.Request {\n\tlatchSpans := &spanset.SpanSet{}\n\tlockSpans := &lockspanset.LockSpanSet{}\n\n\tspan := roachpb.Span{Key: key, EndKey: key.Next()}\n\n\t// Determine latch access based on lock strength\n\tvar latchAccess spanset.SpanAccess\n\tvar latchTs hlc.Timestamp\n\tswitch strength {\n\tcase lock.None, lock.Shared:\n\t\tlatchAccess = spanset.SpanReadOnly\n\t\tlatchTs = timestamp\n\tcase lock.Update, lock.Exclusive, lock.Intent:\n\t\tlatchAccess = spanset.SpanReadWrite\n\t\tlatchTs = timestamp\n\t}\n\n\tlatchSpans.AddMVCC(latchAccess, span, latchTs)\n\tlockSpans.Add(strength, span)\n\n\tba := &kvpb.BatchRequest{}\n\tif txn != nil {\n\t\tba.Txn = txn\n\t}\n\tba.Timestamp = timestamp\n\tba.UserPriority = priority\n\n\treturn concurrency.Request{\n\t\tTxn:        txn,\n\t\tTimestamp:  timestamp,\n\t\tLatchSpans: latchSpans,\n\t\tLockSpans:  lockSpans,\n\t\tBaFmt:      ba,\n\t}\n}\n\n// TestWriteWriteConflict verifies that write-write conflicts are properly\n// detected between two transactions.\nfunc TestWriteWriteConflict(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer should not be allowed to proceed without waiting\n\tif g2 != nil {\n\t\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g2)\n\t\t// If we got a guard, we must have been sequenced\n\t\trequire.True(t, g2 != nil, \"second write request should be blocked by first\")\n\t} else {\n\t\t// If we got a response or error, it's also valid - indicates conflict detected\n\t\trequire.True(t, resp != nil || err != nil, \"second request should be blocked\")\n\t}\n}\n\n// TestReadWriteConflictSerializable verifies that read-write conflicts are\n// properly detected under Serializable isolation level.\nfunc TestTransactionPriorityBasedConflictResolution verifies that transaction\n// priority is properly considered when resolving conflicts.\nfunc TestTransactionPriorityBasedConflictResolution(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n\tdefer log.Scope(t).Close(t)\n\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\tc := newCluster()\n\tc.enableTxnPushes()\n\tm := := uuid.MakeV4()\n\thighPriority := roachpb.HighUserPriority\n\ttxn1 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn1ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: ts,\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   ts,\n\t\t\tPriority:       highPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          ts,\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: ts,\n\t}\n\n\ttxn2ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\tlowPriority := roachpb.LowUserPriority\n\ttxn2 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn2ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: ts,\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   ts,\n\t\t\tPriority:       lowPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          ts,\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: ts,\n\t}\n\n\t// Verify that high priority txn is 9 > low priority txn which is 1\n\trequire.Greater(t, int(highPriority), int(lowPriority))\n\n\t// Create nil, req2, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\tif g2 != nil {\n\t\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g2)\n\t}\n\n\t// Verify that the concurrency manager handled the priority-based conflict\n\trequire.True(t, err != nil || resp != nil || g2 != nil, \"priority-based conflict should be detected\")\n}\n\n// TestLockAcquisitionTimeoutBehavior verifies that lock acquisition respects\n// timeout behavior when waiting on locks.\nfunc TestLockAcquisitionTimeoutBehavior(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n\tdefer log.Scope(t).Close(t)\n\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\tc := newCluster()\n\tc.enableTxnPushes()\n\tm := concurrency.NewManager(c.makeConfig())\n\tm.OnRangeLeaseUpdated(1, true /* isLeaseholder */)\n\n\tts := hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100}\n\n\ttxn1ID := 0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: ts,\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   ts,\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          ts,\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: ts,\n\t}\n\n\ttxn2ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\ttxn2 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn2ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: ts,\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   ts,\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          ts,\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: ts,\n\t}\n\n\t// First request acquires a lock\n\treq1 := makeRequest(txn1, ts, roachpb.Key(\"timeout_key\"), lock.Intent, roachpb.NormalUserPriority)\n\n\tg1, _, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req1, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.NotNil(t, g1)\n\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g1)\n\n\t// Second request attempts to acquire the same lock with a timeout\n\t// We'll use a very short timeout (1 nanosecond) to ensure it expires\n\treq2 := makeRequest(txn2, ts, roachpb.Key(\"timeout_key\"), lock.Intent, roachpb.NormalUserPriority)\n\treq2.LockTimeout = 1 * time.Nanosecond\n\n\t// Create a context with a timeout for the second request\n\tctx2, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, 50*time.Millisecond)\n\tdefer cancel()\n\n\t// Attempt to sequence the second request\n\tg2, resp, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx2, nil, req2, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\tif g2 != nil {\n\t\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx2, g2)\n\t}\n\n\t// The second request should experience some form of timeout or conflict\n\t// At minimum, verify that the concurrency manager handles the timeout\n\trequire.True(t, err != nil || resp != nil || g2 != nil, \"timeout behavior should be handled\")\n}\n\n// TestMaxLockWaitQueueLength verifies that the lock table enforces a maximum\n// wait queue length for lock contention.\nfunc TestMaxLockWaitQueueLength(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n\tdefer log.Scope(t).Close(t)\n\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\tc := newCluster()\n\tc.enableTxnPushes()\n\tm := concurrency.NewManager(c.makeConfig())\n\tm.OnRangeLeaseUpdated(1, true /* isLeaseholder */)\n\n\tts := hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100}\n\n\t// Create a first transaction that will hold a lock\n\ttxn1ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\ttxn1 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn1ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: ts,\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   ts,\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          ts,\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: ts,\n\t}\n\n\t// First request holds a lock\n\treq1 := makeRequest(txn1, ts, roachpb.Key(\"queue_key\"), lock.Intent, roachpb.NormalUserPriority)\n\n\tg1, _, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req1, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.NotNil(t, g1)\n\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g1)\n\n\t// Create multiple transactions trying to acquire the same lock\n\t// to test the wait queue length enforcement\n\tmaxQueueLength := 2\n\tfor i := 0; i < maxQueueLength+2; i++ {\n\t\ttxnID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\t\ttxn := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\t\tID:             txnID,\n\t\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t\tReadTimestamp:          hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t}\n\n\t\treq := concurrency.Request{\n\t\t\tTimestamp:               txn.ReadTimestamp,\n\t\t\tPriority:                roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t\tTxn:                     txn,\n\t\t\tMaxLockWaitQueueLength:  int32(maxQueueLength),\n\t\t\tSpans: concurrency.SpanSet{\n\t\t\t\tSpans: []concurrency.Span{\n\t\t\t\t\t{Key: roachpb.Key(\"queue_key\"), EndKey: nil},\n\t\t\t\t},\n\t\t\t\tBoundarySpan: concurrency.Span{Key: roachpb.Key(\"queue_key\"), EndKey: roachpb.Key(\"queue_key_end\")},\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tg, _, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\t\tif g != nil {\n\t\t\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// After exceeding max queue length, subsequent requests should fail\n\t\tif i >= maxQueueLength {\n\t\t\trequire.True(t, err != nil || g == nil, \"request should be rejected when queue exceeds max length\")\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}\n\n// TestIntentResolutionAfterCommit verifies that intents are properly resolved\n// when a transaction commits.\nfunc TestIntentResolutionAfterCommit(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n\tdefer log.Scope(t).Close(t)\n\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\tc := newCluster()\n\tc.enableTxnPushes()\n\tm := concurrency.NewManager(c.makeConfig())\n\tm.OnRangeLeaseUpdated(1, true /* isLeaseholder */)\n\n\ttxnID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\ttxn := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txnID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t}\n\n\t// Create a write request that will create an intent\n\treq := concurrency.Request{\n\t\tTimestamp: txn.ReadTimestamp,\n\t\tPriority:  roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\tTxn:       txn,\n\t\tSpans: concurrency.SpanSet{\n\t\t\tSpans: []concurrency.Span{\n\t\t\t\t{Key: roachpb.Key(\"intent_key\"), EndKey: nil},\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t\tBoundarySpan: concurrency.Span{Key: roachpb.Key(\"intent_key\"), EndKey: roachpb.Key(\"intent_key_end\")},\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\n\tg, _, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.NotNil(t, g)\n\n\t// Simulate transaction commit by updating the transaction status\n\tcommittedTxn := txn.Clone()\n\tcommittedTxn.Status = roachpb.COMMITTED\n\n\t// Notify the lock table about the committed transaction\n\tm.OnTransactionUpdated(ctx, committedTxn)\n\n\t// Finish the request\n\tm.FinishReq(ctx, g)\n\n\t// At this point, the intent should be resolved in the lock table\n\t// Further requests on the same key should not be blocked by this transaction\n}\n\n// TestIntentResolutionAfterAbort verifies that intents are properly released\n// when a transaction aborts.\nfunc detection works\n// when two transactions wait on each other.\nfunc TestDeadlockDetectionTwoTransactions(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n\tdefer log.Scope(t).Close(t)\n\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\tc := newCluster()\n\tc.enableTxnPushes()\n\tm := concurrency.NewManager(c.makeConfig())\n\tm.OnRangeLeaseUpdated(1, true /* isLeaseholder */)\n\n\t// Create two transactions\n\ttxn1ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\ttxn1 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn1ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t}\n\n\ttxn2ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\ttxn2 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn2ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100},\n\t}\n\n\t// txn1 writes to key \"a\"\n\treq1 := concurrency.Request{\n\t\tTimestamp: txn1.ReadTimestamp,\n\t\tPriority:  roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\tTxn:       txn1,\n\t\tSpans: concurrency.SpanSet{\n\t\t\tSpans: []concurrency.Span{\n\t\t\t\t{Key: roachpb.Key(\"deadlock_a\"), EndKey: nil},\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t\tBoundarySpan: concurrency.Span{Key: roachpb.Key(\"deadlock_a\"), EndKey: roachpb.Key(\"deadlock_a_end\")},\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\n\tg1, _, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req1, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.NotNil(t, g1)\n\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g1)\n\n\t// txn2 writes to key \"b\"\n\treq2 := concurrency.Request{\n\t\tTimestamp: txn2.ReadTimestamp,\n\t\tPriority:  roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\tTxn:       txn2,\n\t\tSpans: concurrency.SpanSet{\n\t\t\tSpans: []concurrency.Span{\n\t\t\t\t{Key: roachpb.Key(\"deadlock_b\"), EndKey: nil},\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t\tBoundarySpan: concurrency.Span{Key: roachpb.Key(\"deadlock_b\"), EndKey: roachpb.Key(\"deadlock_b_end\")},\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\n\tg2, _, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req2, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.NotNil(t, g2)\n\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g2)\n\n\t// txn1 tries to write to key \"b\" (already held by txn2)\n\t// This creates a potential deadlock scenario\n\treq1b := concurrency.Request{\n\t\tTimestamp: txn1.ReadTimestamp,\n\t\tPriority:  roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\tTxn:       txn1,\n\t\tSpans: concurrency.SpanSet{\n\t\t\tSpans: []concurrency.Span{\n\t\t\t\t{Key: roachpb.Key(\"deadlock_b\"), EndKey: nil},\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t\tBoundarySpan: concurrency.Span{Key: roachpb.Key(\"deadlock_b\"), EndKey: roachpb.Key(\"deadlock_b_end\")},\n\t\t},\n\t}\n\n\tg1b, resp1b, err1b := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req1b, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\tif g1b != nil {\n\t\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g1b)\n\t}\n\n\t// The system should detect or handle the potential deadlock scenario\n\t// At minimum, verify that the sequencing completes without panic\n\trequire.True(t, err1b != nil || resp1b != nil || g1b != nil, \"deadlock scenario should be handled\")\n}\n\n// TestConcurrentRequestsOnDifferentKeys */)\n\n\t// Create two transactions\n\ttxn1ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\ttxn1 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn1ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel: Helper function to create a Request with the necessary spans\nfunc makeRequest(\n\ttxn *roachpb.Transaction,\n\ttimestamp hlc.Timestamp,\n\tkey roachpb.Key,\n\tstrength lock.Strength,\n\tpriority roachpb.UserPriority,\n) concurrency.Request {\n\tlatchSpans := &spanset.SpanSet{}\n\tlockSpans := &lockspanset.LockSpanSet{}\n\n\tspan := roachpb.Span{Key: key, EndKey: key.Next()}\n\n\t// Determine latch access based on lock strength\n\tvar latchAccess spanset.SpanAccess\n\tvar latchTs hlc.Timestamp\n\tswitch strength {\n\tcase lock.None, lock.Shared:\n\t\tlatchAccess = spanset.SpanReadOnly\n\t\tlatchTs = timestamp\n\tcase lock.Update, lock.Exclusive, lock.Intent:\n\t\tlatchAccess = spanset.SpanReadWrite\n\t\tlatchTs = timestamp\n\t}\n\n\tlatchSpans.AddMVCC(latchAccess, span, latchTs)\n\tlockSpans.Add(strength, span)\n\n\tba := &kvpb.BatchRequest{}\n\tif txn != nil {\n\t\tba.Txn = txn\n\t}\n\tba.Timestamp = timestamp\n\tba.UserPriority = priority\n\n\treturn concurrency.Request{\n\t\tTxn:        txn,\n\t\tTimestamp:  timestamp,\n\t\tLatchSpans: latchSpans,\n\t\tLockSpans:  lockSpans,\n\t\tBaFmt:      ba,\n\t}\n}\n\n// TestWriteWriteConflict verifies that write-write conflicts are properly\n// detected between two transactions.\nfunc TestWriteWriteConflict(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n\tdefer log.Scope(t).Close(t)\n\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\tc := newCluster()\n\tc.enableTxnPushes()\n\tm := concurrency.NewManager(c.makeConfig())\n\tm.OnRangeLeaseUpdated(1, true /* isLeaseholder */)\n\n\t// Create two transactions\n\ttxn1ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\tts := hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100}\n\ttxn1 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn1ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: ts,\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   ts,\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          ts,\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: ts,\n\t}\n\n\ttxn2ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\ttxn2 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn2ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: ts,\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   ts,\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          ts,\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: ts,\n\t}\n\n\t// Create write request from txn1 nil, req1, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.NotNil(t, g1)\n\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g1)\n\n\t// Create write request from txn2 to the same key \"a\"\n\treq2 := makeRequest(txn2, ts, roachpb.Key(\"a\"), lock.Intent, roachpb.NormalUserPriority)\n\n\t// Attempt to sequence the second transaction's write request\n\t// This should encounter a latch conflict from the first transaction\n\tg2, resp, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req2, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\n\t// The second request should either get a response or error indicating conflict\n\t// It should not be allowed to proceed without waiting\n\tif g2 != nil {\n\t\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g2)\n\t\t// If we got a guard, we must have been sequenced\n\t\trequire.True(t, g2 != nil, \"second write request should be blocked by first\")\n\t} else {\n\t\t// If we got a response or error, it's also valid - indicates conflict detected\n\t\trequire.True(t, resp != nil || err != nil, \"second request should be blocked\")\n\t}\n}\n\n// TestReadWriteConflictSerializable verifies that read-write conflicts are\n// properly detected under Serializable isolation level.\nfunc Helper function to create a Request with the necessary spans\nfunc makeRequest(\n\ttxn *roachpb.Transaction,\n\ttimestamp hlc.Timestamp,\n\tkey roachpb.Key,\n\tstrength lock.Strength,\n\tpriority roachpb.UserPriority,\n) concurrency.Request {\n\tlatchSpans := &spanset.SpanSet{}\n\tlockSpans := &lockspanset.LockSpanSet{}\n\n\tspan := roachpb.Span{Key: key, EndKey: key.Next()}\n\n\t// Determine latch access based on lock strength\n\tvar latchAccess spanset.SpanAccess\n\tvar latchTs hlc.Timestamp\n\tswitch strength {\n\tcase lock.None, lock.Shared:\n\t\tlatchAccess = spanset.SpanReadOnly\n\t\tlatchTs = timestamp\n\tcase lock.Update, lock.Exclusive, lock.Intent:\n\t\tlatchAccess = spanset.SpanReadWrite\n\t\tlatchTs = timestamp\n\t}\n\n\tlatchSpans.AddMVCC(latchAccess, span, latchTs)\n\tlockSpans.Add(strength, span)\n\n\tba := &kvpb.BatchRequest{}\n\tif txn != nil {\n\t\tba.Txn = txn\n\t}\n\tba.Timestamp = timestamp\n\tba.UserPriority = priority\n\n\treturn concurrency.Request{\n\t\tTxn:        txn,\n\t\tTimestamp:  timestamp,\n\t\tLatchSpans: latchSpans,\n\t\tLockSpans:  lockSpans,\n\t\tBaFmt:      ba,\n\t}\n}\n\n// TestWriteWriteConflict verifies that write-write conflicts are properly\n// detected between two transactions.\nfunc TestWriteWriteConflict(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n\tdefer log.Scope(t).Close(t)\n\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\tc := newCluster()\n\tc.enableTxnPushes()\n\tm := concurrency.NewManager(c.makeConfig())\n\tm.OnRangeLeaseUpdated(1, true /* isLeaseholder */)\n\n\t// Create two transactions\n\ttxn1ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\tts := hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100}\n\ttxn1 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn1ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: ts,\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   ts,\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          ts,\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: ts,\n\t}\n\n\ttxn2ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\ttxn2 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn2ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: ts,\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   ts,\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          ts,\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: ts,\n\t}\n\n\t// Create write request from txn1 nil, req1, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.NotNil(t, g1)\n\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g1)\n\n\t// Create write request from txn2 to the same key \"a\"\n\treq2 := makeRequest(txn2, ts, roachpb.Key(\"a\"), lock.Intent, roachpb.NormalUserPriority)\n\n\t// Attempt to sequence the second transaction's write request\n\t// This should encounter a latch conflict from the first transaction\n\tg2, resp, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req2, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\n\t// The second request should either get a response or error indicating conflict\n\t// It should not be allowed to proceed without waiting\n\tif g2 != nil {\n\t\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g2)\n\t\t// If we got a guard, we must have been sequenced\n\t\trequire.True(t, g2 != nil, \"second write request should be blocked by first\")\n\t} else {\n\t\t// If we got a response or error, it's also valid - indicates conflict detected\n\t\trequire.True(t, resp != nil || err != nil, \"second request should be blocked\")\n\t}\n}\n\n// TestReadWriteConflictSerializable verifies that read-write conflicts are\n// properly detected under Serializable isolation level.\nfunc enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\t\tID:             txnID,\n\t\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: ts,\n\t\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   ts,\n\t\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t\tReadTimestamp:          ts,\n\t\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: ts,\n\t\t}\n\n\t\treq := makeRequest(txn, ts, Helper function to create a Request with the necessary spans\nfunc makeRequest(\n\ttxn *roachpb.Transaction,\n\ttimestamp hlc.Timestamp,\n\tkey roachpb.Key,\n\tstrength lock.Strength,\n\tpriority roachpb.UserPriority,\n) concurrency.Request {\n\tlatchSpans := &spanset.SpanSet{}\n\tlockSpans := &lockspanset.LockSpanSet{}\n\n\tspan := roachpb.Span{Key: key, EndKey: key.Next()}\n\n\t// Determine latch access based on lock strength\n\tvar latchAccess spanset.SpanAccess\n\tvar latchTs hlc.Timestamp\n\tswitch strength {\n\tcase lock.None, lock.Shared:\n\t\tlatchAccess = spanset.SpanReadOnly\n\t\tlatchTs = timestamp\n\tcase lock.Update, lock.Exclusive, lock.Intent:\n\t\tlatchAccess = spanset.SpanReadWrite\n\t\tlatchTs = timestamp\n\t}\n\n\tlatchSpans.AddMVCC(latchAccess, span, latchTs)\n\tlockSpans.Add(strength, span)\n\n\tba := &kvpb.BatchRequest{}\n\tif txn != nil {\n\t\tba.Txn = txn\n\t}\n\tba.Timestamp = timestamp\n\tba.UserPriority = priority\n\n\treturn concurrency.Request{\n\t\tTxn:        txn,\n\t\tTimestamp:  timestamp,\n\t\tLatchSpans: latchSpans,\n\t\tLockSpans:  lockSpans,\n\t\tBaFmt:      ba,\n\t}\n}\n\n// TestWriteWriteConflict verifies that write-write conflicts are properly\n// detected between two transactions.\nfunc TestWriteWriteConflict(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n\tdefer log.Scope(t).Close(t)\n\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\tc := newCluster()\n\tc.enableTxnPushes()\n\tm := concurrency.NewManager(c.makeConfig())\n\tm.OnRangeLeaseUpdated(1, true /* isLeaseholder */)\n\n\t// Create two transactions\n\ttxn1ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\tts := hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100}\n\ttxn1 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn1ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: ts,\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   ts,\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          ts,\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: ts,\n\t}\n\n\ttxn2ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\ttxn2 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn2ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: ts,\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   ts,\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          ts,\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: ts,\n\t}\n\n\t// Create write request from txn1 to key \"a\"\n\treq1 := makeRequest(txn1, ts, roachpb.Key(\"a\"), lock.Intent, roachpb.NormalUserPriority)\n\n\t// Sequence the first transaction's write request\n\tg1, _, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req1, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.NotNil(t, g1)\n\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g1)\n\n\t// Create write request from txn2 to the same key \"a\"\n\treq2 := makeRequest(txn2, ts, roachpb.Key(\"a\"), lock.Intent, roachpb.NormalUserPriority)\n\n\t// Attempt to sequence the second transaction's write request\n\t// This should encounter a latch conflict from the first transaction\n\tg2, resp, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req2, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\n\t// The second request should either get a response or error indicating conflict\n\t// It should not be allowed to proceed without waiting\n\tif g2 != nil {\n\t\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g2)\n\t\t// If we got a guard, we must have been sequenced\n\t\trequire.True(t, g2 != nil, \"second write request should be blocked by first\")\n\t} else {\n\t\t// If we got a response or error, it's also valid - indicates conflict detected\n\t\trequire.True(t, resp != nil || err != nil, \"second request should be blocked\")\n\t}\n}\n\n// TestReadWriteConflictSerializable verifies that read-write conflicts are\n// properly detected under Serializable isolation level.\nfunc isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: ts,\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   ts,\n\t\t\tPriority:       highPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp: 0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: ts,\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   ts,\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          ts,\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: ts,\n\t}\n\n\ttxn2ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\ttxn2 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn2ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: ts,\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   ts,\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          ts,\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: ts,\n\t}\n\n\t// First handled\")\n}\n\n// TestMaxLockWaitQueueLength verifies that the lock table enforces a maximum\n// wait queue length for lock contention.\nfunc TestMaxLockWaitQueueLength(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n\tdefer log.Scope(t).Close(t)\n\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\tc := newCluster()\n\tc.enableTxnPushes()\n\tm := 0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: ts,\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   ts,\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          ts,\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: ts,\n\t}\n\n\t// First request holds a lock\n\treq1 := makeRequest(txn1, ts, roachpb.Key(\"queue_key\"), lock.Intent, roachpb.NormalUserPriority)\n\n\tg1, _, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req1, Helper function to create a Request with the necessary spans\nfunc makeRequest(\n\ttxn *roachpb.Transaction,\n\ttimestamp hlc.Timestamp,\n\tkey roachpb.Key,\n\tstrength lock.Strength,\n\tpriority roachpb.UserPriority,\n) concurrency.Request {\n\tlatchSpans := &spanset.SpanSet{}\n\tlockSpans := &lockspanset.LockSpanSet{}\n\n\tspan := roachpb.Span{Key: key, EndKey: key.Next()}\n\n\t// Determine latch access based on lock strength\n\tvar latchAccess spanset.SpanAccess\n\tvar latchTs hlc.Timestamp\n\tswitch strength {\n\tcase lock.None, lock.Shared:\n\t\tlatchAccess = spanset.SpanReadOnly\n\t\tlatchTs = timestamp\n\tcase lock.Update, lock.Exclusive, lock.Intent:\n\t\tlatchAccess = spanset.SpanReadWrite\n\t\tlatchTs = timestamp\n\t}\n\n\tlatchSpans.AddMVCC(latchAccess, span, latchTs)\n\tlockSpans.Add(strength, span)\n\n\tba := &kvpb.BatchRequest{}\n\tif txn != nil {\n\t\tba.Txn = txn\n\t}\n\tba.Timestamp = timestamp\n\tba.UserPriority = priority\n\n\treturn concurrency.Request{\n\t\tTxn:        txn,\n\t\tTimestamp:  timestamp,\n\t\tLatchSpans: latchSpans,\n\t\tLockSpans:  lockSpans,\n\t\tBaFmt:      ba,\n\t}\n}\n\n// TestWriteWriteConflict verifies that write-write conflicts are properly\n// detected between two transactions.\nfunc TestWriteWriteConflict(t *testing.T) {\n\tdefer leaktest.AfterTest(t)()\n\tdefer log.Scope(t).Close(t)\n\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\tc := newCluster()\n\tc.enableTxnPushes()\n\tm := concurrency.NewManager(c.makeConfig())\n\tm.OnRangeLeaseUpdated(1, true /* isLeaseholder */)\n\n\t// Create two transactions\n\ttxn1ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\tts := hlc.Timestamp{WallTime: 100}\n\ttxn1 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn1ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: ts,\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   ts,\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          ts,\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: ts,\n\t}\n\n\ttxn2ID := uuid.MakeV4()\n\ttxn2 := &roachpb.Transaction{\n\t\tTxnMeta: enginepb.TxnMeta{\n\t\t\tID:             txn2ID,\n\t\t\tIsoLevel:       isolation.Serializable(),\n\t\t\tEpoch:          0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: ts,\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   ts,\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          ts,\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: ts,\n\t}\n\n\t// Create write request from txn1 to key \"a\"\n\treq1 := makeRequest(txn1, ts, roachpb.Key(\"a\"), lock.Intent, roachpb.NormalUserPriority)\n\n\t// Sequence the first transaction's write request\n\tg1, _, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req1, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.NotNil(t, g1)\n\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g1)\n\n\t// Create write request from txn2 to the same key \"a\"\n\treq2 := makeRequest(txn2, ts, roachpb.Key(\"a\"), lock.Intent, roachpb.NormalUserPriority)\n\n\t// Attempt to sequence the second transaction's write request\n\t// This should encounter a latch conflict from the first transaction\n\tg2, resp, err := m.SequenceReq(ctx, nil, req2, concurrency.PessimisticEval)\n\n\t// The second request should either get a response or error indicating conflict\n\t// It should not be allowed to proceed without waiting\n\tif g2 != nil {\n\t\tdefer m.FinishReq(ctx, g2)\n\t\t// If we got a guard, we must have been sequenced\n\t\trequire.True(t, g2 != nil, \"second write request should be blocked by first\")\n\t} else {\n\t\t// If we got a response or error, it's also valid - indicates conflict detected\n\t\trequire.True(t, resp != nil || err != nil, \"second request should be blocked\")\n\t}\n}\n\n// TestReadWriteConflictSerializable verifies that read-write conflicts are\n// properly detected under Serializable isolation level.\nfunc 0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: ts,\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   ts,\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          ts,\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: ts,\n\t}\n\n\ttxn2ID 0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: ts,\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   ts,\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          ts,\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: ts,\n\t}\n\n\t// First 0,\n\t\t\tWriteTimestamp: ts,\n\t\t\tMinTimestamp:   ts,\n\t\t\tPriority:       roachpb.NormalUserPriority,\n\t\t},\n\t\tReadTimestamp:          ts,\n\t\tGlobalUncertaintyLimit: ts,\n\t}\n\n\t// First
